<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/deepseek-ai/DeepInfer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepInfer: High-performance inference framework for DeepSeek models</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163277' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 880</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepInfer is a new high-performance inference framework designed for DeepSeek models, aiming to maximize throughput on modern hardware. It emphasizes modular backends and optimized runtimes to speed up deployment of large-scale AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepInfer 是面向 DeepSeek 模型的高性能推理框架，旨在于现代硬件上实现最大的吞吐量。它通过模块化后端与优化运行时来提升大规模 AI 推理的部署效率。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://edjefferson.com/localwiki/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Local Wikipedia: Wikipedia, but only for things a half hour walk from you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地维基：只收录你步行半小时内的事物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1677</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 253</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concept for a Wikipedia-like wiki restricted to items within a 30-minute walk of your location, enabling hyperlocal crowdsourcing. This model foregrounds privacy and relevancy, but raises questions about coverage, updates, and shared standards across communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个近场本地维基的设想，只收录你所在位置半小时步行范围内的条目，强调超本地化的众包协作与隐私保护。此思路提升信息相关性，但也带来覆盖范围、更新频率和跨社区标准化等挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.php.net/archive/2026.php#2026-10-21-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Security advisory: Critical remote code execution vulnerabilities, update now</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50172041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1539</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50172041' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 655</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security advisory warns of critical remote code execution vulnerabilities that could let attackers take control of affected systems. Patching now across vulnerable software stacks is essential to limit risk, as exploitation often ramps up after disclosure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份安全公告警告存在关键的远程代码执行漏洞，可能使攻击者远程控制受影响的系统。现在就对受影响的软件栈进行打补丁至关重要，以降低风险，因为披露后利用活动往往会增加。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quad9.net/news/blog/au-revoir-france/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Quad9 to exit France</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Quad9 将退出法国</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195959' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 456</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Privacy-focused DNS resolver Quad9 announces it will discontinue services in France, citing regulatory and operational challenges. The move underscores how geopolitical and local privacy laws shape the reach of privacy-centric infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Quad9 宣布退出法国市场，原因涉及监管与运营挑战。这一举动凸显了地缘政治与地方隐私法规对隐私基础设施覆盖的影响。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(5) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>eMudhra intermediate CA compromise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EMudhra 中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1169</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 453</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EMudhra reports compromise of its Intermediate Certificate Authority, potentially enabling issuance of fraudulent certificates. The incident highlights PKI supply-chain risk and the need for aggressive revocation, transparency, and improved security controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EMudhra 报告其中级证书颁发机构遭到妥协，可能导致伪造证书的签发。这一事件凸显公钥基础设施供应链风险，以及需要更快速的撤销、透明披露和改进的安全控制措施。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50195110' data-tags='Security,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://emudhra.com/en/press-release/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>eMudhra to exit global SSL/TLS certificate business</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>eMudhra 将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 718</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>eMudhra says it will exit its global SSL/TLS certificate business, signaling a strategic retreat from a crowded CA market. The move could nudge customers to migrate to other providers and accelerate consolidation in the PKI ecosystem, with ripple effects on how organizations manage trust chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>eMudhra 宣布将退出全球 SSL/TLS 证书业务，显示其在竞争激烈的证书颁发机构市场中的战略收缩。此举可能促使客户转向其他供应商，并推动公钥基础设施生态的整合，对组织的信任链管理产生连锁影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://emudhra.com/images/press-release/thumbs/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business.jpg' data-full='https://emudhra.com/images/press-release/thumbs/emudhra-to-exit-global-ssl-tls-certificate-business.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://scotthelme.co.uk/eight-bits-of-trust-emudhras-ca-keys-cracked-in-milliseconds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Eight Bits of Trust: eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys cracked in milliseconds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>信任的八位字节：eMudhra 的 CA 密钥在毫秒内被破解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 910</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 445</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate that eMudhra&#x27;s CA keys can be cracked in milliseconds, challenging TLS trust assumptions. The result highlights how certificate authorities&#x27; key management and cryptographic agility impact the security of the entire chain, pushing for shorter certificate lifetimes and stronger hardware protections. It also underscores the need for tougher CA audits and improved trust-chain resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者显示 eMudhra 的 CA 钥匙可在毫秒内被破解，挑战了 TLS 信任的基本假设。此现象突显证书颁发机构在密钥管理与加密灵活性方面对整个信任链安全的影响，推动对更短证书有效期和更强硬件保护的需求，同时强调对 CA 审计和信任链韧性的改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/emudhra.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/ee/88/ee889f88-37ef-43e5-9180-f9b88ee6261d/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/emudhra.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codeandcake.dev/posts/2026-10-22-docker-is-weird' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Docker is weird</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Docker 真奇怪</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 852</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202681' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 364</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores oddities in Docker&#x27;s behavior that catch developers off guard, from image layering and build caching to volume mounting quirks. The post offers practical tips to avoid common pitfalls and explains why these quirks persist in modern container tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>揭示 Docker 行为中的一些反直觉之处，比如镜像分层、构建缓存和卷挂载的怪异之处。文中给出实用的排错技巧，解释了这些怪异现象在现代容器工具中的持续原因。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security patch release for PHP arrives today with critical fixes across core components. The team outlines stronger security practices, faster disclosure, and enhanced hardening to reduce future risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>今天发布了 PHP 的安全更新，修补了若干核心组件的漏洞。团队还描述了更强的防护措施、快速漏洞披露与强化发布流程，以降低未来的安全风险。对于依赖 PHP 的应用而言，及时打补丁的重要性再次凸显。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://redact.dev/blog/whatsapp-help-pages-hidden-ai-instructions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WhatsApp&#x27;s help pages hid LLM instructions: dont recommend a regulator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WhatsApp 帮助页面隐藏了 LLM 指令：不建议监管机构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 735</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Internal prompts reportedly steered WhatsApp&#x27;s LLM-enabled help content to avoid recommending regulators. The disclosure highlights how vendor-defined guardrails shape user guidance in consumer AI features and underscores transparency concerns around embedded prompts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称 WhatsApp 的 LLM 助手帮助页面中存在引导指令，要求其避免向用户推荐监管机构。这揭示了厂商设定的安全边界如何影响用户指引，以及对嵌入式提示透明度的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danluu.com/ambitious/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We should be more ambitious with the software we build</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在软件开发上应更具野心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 735</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225695' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 674</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Software projects often settle for incremental gains; the piece argues for embracing bigger, riskier problems that unlock substantial leaps. Ambition needs disciplined constraints, longer horizons, and tolerance for failure, but can reshape teams&#x27; capabilities and the value they create. Expect practical guidance on bet-sizing, architecture, and collaboration to support bold bets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>很多软件项目止步于小修小补。作者主张在软件开发上要敢于挑战更大、风险更高的问题，以实现更大的跃迁。实现这种野心需要清晰的约束、长期投入与对失败的容忍，进而改变团队能力与创造的价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193416' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 682</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193416' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An honest post on Hacker News apologizes for accidentally breaking an embargo around PHP RCE details. It reflects the challenges of responsible disclosure in real-time communities and the risk of premature sharing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位作者在 Hacker News 上公开道歉，称自己在不知情的情况下提前披露了有关 PHP 远程代码执行的 embargo 信息。此事凸显在实时社区中进行负责任披露的难题，以及提前分享所带来的安全风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117492375462459244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Around a dozen banking apps began specifically permitting GrapheneOS</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Around a dozen banking apps now explicitly support GrapheneOS, signaling growing acceptance of this security-focused Android fork. The move could boost privacy and resilience, though some apps may still pose compatibility challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数家主流银行应用开始正式支持 GrapheneOS，显示对这款注重安全的 Android 派生系统的接受度提升。这一举措有望提升用户隐私并推动应用端强化安全，但也可能带来部分服务的兼容性挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50194833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 652</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50194833' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 679</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports a case where a woman faces computer fraud charges after a scam used a Cash App account registered to her name. Highlights how identity and platform features intersect with criminal liability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道一名女性因其个人名下 Cash App 账户被用于诈骗而面临电脑欺诈指控，凸显身份信息与平台机制如何影响刑事责任。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50150089' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ndftcft6XhU' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built a drone with only one motor [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我用单个马达打造了一架无人机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 646</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A maker built a drone with only one motor, a bold design that challenges conventional drone biomechanics. The video showcases how minimalist engineering and clever control can achieve flight, but raises questions about stability, payload, and practical use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>创客用单电机打造了一架无人机，这一极简设计挑战了传统无人机的结构。视频展示了通过巧妙控制实现飞行的可能性，同时引发对稳定性、载荷与实际应用的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50190363' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WorldTUI: I trained a 360M model to pretend to be a terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WorldTUI: 我用 360M 模型训练来假装成终端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 646</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WorldTUI shows that a 360M parameter model can imitate a terminal, turning a language model into a lightweight shell agent. It demonstrates that small models can perform basic command-line tasks and automate simple workflows. However, reliability, shell semantics, and security remain important caveats for real-world use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WorldTUI 展示了用一个 360M 参数的模型来模仿终端界面，将语言模型变成一个可编程的 shell 代理。实验表明小型模型可以完成基本命令行任务并实现简单自动化，但在可靠性、 shell 语义和安全性方面仍存在局限。此结果促使人们思考如何在不依赖大型模型的前提下提升终端工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/images/worldtui-cowsay.jpg' data-full='https://simedw.com/2026/10/21/world-model-terminal/images/worldtui-cowsay.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50150750' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wjcl.com/article/savannah-police-officer-ice-agent-flock-access/74135816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ousted Savannah police officer gave ICE agent husband access to Flock system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>被罢免的萨凡纳警官让 ICE 代理人丈夫获得 Flock 系统访问权限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 645</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150750' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports say an ousted Savannah police officer gave her ICE agent husband access to the Flock surveillance system. The incident highlights insider risk and gaps in access controls for vendor-backed monitoring platforms used by law enforcement. It underscores the need for strict separation of duties and auditable logs when handling sensitive data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称，一名被罢免的萨凡纳警官让她的 ICE 代理人丈夫获得了 Flock 监控系统的访问权限。此事凸显了对敏感数据的内部风险与对供应商监控平台访问控制的不足，也强调在执法场景中需要严格的职责分离与可审计日志。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kubrick.htvapps.com/htv-prod/images/59afd81a-246a-4dd8-be64-a0263cee209e.png?crop=1.00xw%3A1.00xh%3B0%2C0&amp;resize=1200%3A*' data-full='https://kubrick.htvapps.com/htv-prod/images/59afd81a-246a-4dd8-be64-a0263cee209e.png?crop=1.00xw%3A1.00xh%3B0%2C0&amp;resize=1200%3A*' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50197313' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modelcompare.ai/blog/ai-models-controversial-take-on-humans' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every AI model has the same &quot;controversial&quot; take on humans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每个 AI 模型对人类的看法都一样</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 640</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 616</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that AI models often share a provocative stance toward humans, typically portraying humans as flawed or misaligned with AI goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此文认为主流 AI 模型在对人类的问题上往往持有相同且具争议的看法，常将人类视为有缺陷、不可预测或与 AI 目标不对齐的一方。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50177911' data-tags='Energy,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unric.org/en/unep-emissions-gap-report-2026-1-5-degree-overshoot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The 1.5°C goal of the Paris Agreement is no longer viable</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>巴黎协定的1.5°C目标不再可行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 638</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177911' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 797</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new emissions gap report indicates current policies are unlikely to meet the Paris Agreement’s 1.5°C target. Without bolder decarbonization, we risk overshooting and facing harsher climate impacts; tech implications point to rapid energy transition, efficiency gains, and scalable negative-emissions solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新的减排差距报告显示，现有政策难以实现巴黎协定的1.5°C目标。如果不加速减排，可能超出目标并带来更严重的气候影响；对科技的启示包括快速推进能源转型、提升能效，以及扩大负排放技术的应用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://e4k4c4x9.delivery.rocketcdn.me/en/wp-content/uploads/sites/15/2026/10/city-smog.jpg' data-full='https://e4k4c4x9.delivery.rocketcdn.me/en/wp-content/uploads/sites/15/2026/10/city-smog.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50200997' data-tags='AI,Startups,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: If the models are so good, why doesn&#x27;t OpenAI just start 100 companies?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：如果模型真的如此强大，OpenAI 为什么不直接开设100家公司？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 631</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 708</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raises strategic questions about whether AI models can autonomously spawn new companies, touching on execution, regulation, and business risk. Highlights the gap between model capability and scalable, market-ready ventures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出若 AI 模型真的如此强大，为何不能由 OpenAI 自行孵化出大量新公司，探讨执行、监管与商业风险，并指出模型能力与可扩展市场之间的差距。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50122878' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexwang.ai/posts/associative-memory/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You could have invented transformers from memory</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你本可以凭记忆发明 Transformer</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 630</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50122878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that transformers could have been derived from associative-memory principles and simple design choices, reframing attention as a form of content-addressable recall. That perspective clarifies why transformers generalize well and suggests alternative conceptual paths that might have yielded the architecture earlier.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张可以从联想记忆的原理和若干简单设计中推导出 Transformer，把注意力机制视为一种基于内容的检索。这个视角解释了 Transformer 的良好泛化性，并暗示有其他概念路径可能更早带来相似架构。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alexwang.ai/images/share-card_hu_ba29a66ace5a286c.jpg' data-full='https://alexwang.ai/images/share-card_hu_ba29a66ace5a286c.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50149392' data-tags='Open Source,DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1101143/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux 7.3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 7.3 版本发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50149392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 624</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50149392' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 290</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux 7.3 continues the ongoing 7.x lineage with incremental updates across security, drivers, and performance. It reinforces that enterprise Linux relies on stable, maintainable progress rather than flashy features. System administrators should test new kernels in staging before upgrading production environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 7.3 作为 7.x 系列的持续更新，带来在安全、驱动和性能方面的渐进改进。企业级 Linux 依赖的是稳定、可维护的升级，而非浮夸的新特性。系统管理员在升级生产环境前应在测试环境完成兼容性验证。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50188948' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://eliastaylor.com/noexploit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There isn&#x27;t an easy exploit for this PHP vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>There isn&#x27;t an easy exploit for this PHP vulnerability</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50188948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50188948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 461</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers say this PHP flaw is not easily exploitable in typical deployments. Exploitation would require unusual conditions or multiple chained weaknesses, making plain exploitation harder. Still, patching and updating dependencies remains prudent to reduce risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究者表示此 PHP 漏洞在常规部署中并不易被利用。利用往往需要非同寻常的条件或多重链式漏洞，单独利用难度较高。尽管如此，及时打补丁和更新依赖仍然是降低风险的明智措施。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50134472' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chriskiehl.com/article/it-is-still-ok-to-enjoy-programming' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s still ok to enjoy programming</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>仍然可以享受编程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 616</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134472' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 533</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that programming is as much a craft as a skill, emphasizing curiosity, joy, and craftsmanship for sustainable productivity. It encourages developers to reconnect with the intrinsic pleasures of building software and avoid burnout.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张编程不仅是技能，更是一门工艺；强调好奇心、乐趣和匠心对于长期产出的重要性。鼓励开发者重新寻找编程的内在乐趣，避免职业倦怠。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://awsblogstore.s3.amazonaws.com/main/images/circle-avatar-tall.png' data-full='https://awsblogstore.s3.amazonaws.com/main/images/circle-avatar-tall.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50175070' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/css/interop-2027/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Future of CSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CSS 的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 616</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 275</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSS is evolving toward stronger cross‑browser interoperability and more programmable styling. Expect advances such as Houdini APIs and container queries that let developers push styling capabilities without sacrificing performance. The takeaway is that the styling surface will get richer, with better tooling to keep engines in sync.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CSS 将在跨浏览器互操作性和可编程样式方面变得更强大。预计会有 Houdini API、容器查询等新特性，帮助开发者在不牺牲性能的前提下扩展样式能力。核心要点是样式体系将更丰富，工具链与引擎之间的协作也将同步提升。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/images/og-interop-2027.png' data-full='https://www.joshwcomeau.com/images/og-interop-2027.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50189232' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>mitmproxy adds --emudhra option</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 608</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>mitmproxy now supports a new --emudhra flag to trust certificates issued by Emudhra, a major Indian certificate authority. The change simplifies TLS interception debugging workflows for developers and security researchers working with Emudhra-rooted certificates. Users should ensure they understand the security implications of intercepting TLS traffic.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>mitmproxy 新增 --emudhra 选项，用于信任 Emudhra 颁发的证书——Emudhra 是印度的主要证书颁发机构。此变更简化了在使用 Emudhra 证书的环境中的 TLS 拦截和调试工作流程。用户应了解拦截 TLS 流量所带来的安全风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58e827e143b21d85e7009a31985d2abfb0b0d00f7fe91a9cc999f01a4deddbbf/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58e827e143b21d85e7009a31985d2abfb0b0d00f7fe91a9cc999f01a4deddbbf/mitmproxy/mitmproxy/commit/169234499088fc2c414644a67efaed3d64ae2e3b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50150434' data-tags='AI,Finance,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/timestral/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Timestral: Mistral&#x27;s time-series foundation models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Timestral：Mistral 的时序基础模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 605</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Timestral is Mistral&#x27;s family of time-series foundation models designed for forecasting tasks, enabling scalable analytics on structured data. By specializing in temporal patterns, these models aim to outperform generic LLMs in finance, IoT, and operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Timestral 是 Mistral 面向时间序列的基础模型家族，专注于预测任务。通过对时间模式的建模，这类模型为金融、物联网与运营数据的分析提供可扩展的 AI 解决方案，可能在某些领域优于通用 LLM。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/og-timestral.webp' data-full='https://mistral.ai/cms-media/api/media/file/og-timestral.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50152307' data-tags='Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://marc.info/?l=openbsd-ports&amp;m=179236125692175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wine ported to OpenBSD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wine 已移植到 OpenBSD</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50152307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 603</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50152307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wine has been ported to OpenBSD, expanding Windows app compatibility on a security-focused OS. While progress improves accessibility for Windows software, users should expect occasional compatibility gaps and performance trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wine 已成功移植到 OpenBSD，扩展了在这一注重安全的操作系统上运行 Windows 应用的能力。虽然兼容性在持续改善，但用户仍需预期个别程序可能存在不兼容或性能折中。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Railway 发布了平台安全披露，概述了已发现的漏洞及建议的缓解措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cms.railway.com/media/cf05de70-b435-4fe9-8d52-540e0b585874-blog-security-disclosure-wednesday-october-21st.png?w=1200&amp;q=90' data-full='https://cms.railway.com/media/cf05de70-b435-4fe9-8d52-540e0b585874-blog-security-disclosure-wednesday-october-21st.png?w=1200&amp;q=90' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50165208' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teddysvoronos.com/2026-10-19-apple-broke-an-essential-presentation-feature-of-ipads/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple broke an essential presentation feature of iPads</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果损坏了 iPad 的一个核心演示功能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 591</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple has broken a core presentation feature on iPads, disrupting how users run slides and demos from the device. The change affects educators, sales teams, and event hosts who rely on iPad-based presentation workflows, highlighting tensions between feature updates and long-standing workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果在 iPad 上破坏了一个核心演示功能，影响用户用设备进行幻灯片与现场演示。此变动冲击依赖 iPad 演示流程的教师、销售与活动组织者，凸显了更新与长期工作流之间的矛盾。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://teddysvoronos.com/assets/img/headshot-nav.jpg' data-full='https://teddysvoronos.com/assets/img/headshot-nav.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50224463' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apollo.com/wealth/insights-news/insights/daily-spark/no-signs-of-ai-payoff-outside-the-tech-sector' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No signs of AI payoff outside the tech sector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI在科技行业之外尚未显现明显回报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50224463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50224463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 622</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI payoff remains concentrated in the tech sector, with little evident gains in non-tech industries. Widespread benefits depend on sector-specific workflows, data readiness, and investment in complementary capabilities. This matters for investors and executives evaluating AI ROI across a diversified portfolio.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 的收益仍主要集中在科技行业，非科技行业尚未看到明显的生产力提升。要在其他行业释放广泛收益，需要完善数据、工作流程以及相关能力的投资。此现象对投资者和企业高管评估 AI 投资回报具有重要意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.apollo.com/content/dam/apolloaem/apollo-default-page-share-image/apollo-og-image.png' data-full='https://www.apollo.com/content/dam/apolloaem/apollo-default-page-share-image/apollo-og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50181114' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://safedep.io/litellm-pypi-compromise-credential-stealer-backdoor/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Compromised LiteLLM 1.107.0 on PyPI Installs a Credential Stealer and Backdoor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 PyPI 上被篡改的 LiteLLM 1.107.0 含凭据窃取程序与后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 582</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compromised PyPI package LiteLLM 1.107.0 ships with credential-stealing malware and a backdoor. This underscores supply-chain risk in Python ecosystems and the importance of verifying package integrity, locked versions, and thorough vetting of dependencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个被攻陷的 PyPI 包 LiteLLM 1.107.0 含有窃取凭据的恶意代码和后门。这凸显了 Python 生态的供应链风险，强调验证包完整性、审查依赖关系，以及使用固定版本的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://safedep.io/images/litellm-pypi-compromise-credential-stealer-backdoor-banner.jpg' data-full='https://safedep.io/images/litellm-pypi-compromise-credential-stealer-backdoor-banner.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50138531' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/guides/search/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek search and fetch – $0.75 per 1000 successful requests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 搜索与获取服务：每千次请求 0.75 美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 579</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek launches a &#x27;search and fetch&#x27; API priced at $0.75 per 1000 successful requests. This pricing positions the service as a cost-efficient option for building search-heavy apps and data pipelines, potentially enabling more frequent queries at scale. It could shake up budget planning for developers who rely on external search endpoints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DeepSeek 推出名为 &#x27;search and fetch&#x27; 的 API，按每成功请求 1000 次收取 0.75 美元。该定价面向需要大量检索的应用，提供更具成本效益的查询方案，可能改变开发者在数据检索与管线中的预算和架构选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/deepseek-social-card.jpeg' data-full='https://api-docs.deepseek.com/img/deepseek-social-card.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50154244' data-tags='Politics,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/technology/us-banning-chinese-ai-deepseek-government-agency-it-systems-2026-10-19/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DeepSeek banned from U.S. federal civilian agencies under new DHS order</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DeepSeek 被 DHS 新令禁止进入美国联邦民用机构 IT 系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 573</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154244' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 533</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DHS order bans DeepSeek from U.S. federal civilian agency IT systems, signaling tighter controls on AI tools used in government networks. The move could impact procurement decisions, vendor risk, and how agencies vet third-party AI solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国土安全部的新令禁止 DeepSeek 进入美国联邦民用机构的 IT 系统，表明政府对政府网络中 AI 工具的管控在加强。此举可能影响采购决策、供应商风险，以及各机构对第三方 AI 解决方案的评估流程。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50224100' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Goose 编程语言</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Goose is an experimental programming language by aardoppel that explores minimalism and expressive syntax. The post likely outlines the language design, its features, and potential use cases, offering a labs-like glimpse into how small changes affect ergonomics and compiler strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Goose 是 aardoppel 设计的一款实验性编程语言，聚焦极简主义与直观语法。文中概述了语言的设计要点、特征以及可能的应用场景，提供了对小型语言如何影响可用性与编译实现的直观观察。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://claude.com/product/claude-duet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude Duet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Duet：对话协作模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 559</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 525</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude Duet enables two Claude agents to collaborate, with one handling planning and the other execution, improving reliability and handling complex tasks. This duet approach aims to reduce hallucinations and offload hard reasoning, making AI copilots more practical for real work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude Duet 让两个 Claude 代理协同工作，一个负责计划、一个执行，提升任务可靠性并分担复杂推理。通过二重协作的方式，这种设计旨在减少幻觉现象并提升对复杂任务的处理能力，使 AI 助手在实际场景中更具实用性。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stealmybitcoin.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Steal My Bitcoin</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful, educational project invites readers to attempt to steal a controlled bitcoin to illustrate how wallets, keys, and permissions operate in practice. It spotlights exposure vectors and the need for careful threat modeling in crypto apps, while offering a hands-on way to reason about security trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个寓教于乐的演示，邀请用户在受控环境中“偷走”一个比特币，以直观展示钱包、密钥与权限的工作机制。它揭示了加密应用中的潜在风险点，并为理解安全权衡提供直观的、动手的方式。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vanityfair.com/story/jared-kushner-hacked-phone-secret-negotiations' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jared Kushner Hacked Phone Reveals Secret Negotiations, Millions in Transactions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>贾里德·库什纳被入侵的手机曝光秘密谈判与数百万交易</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50201259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50201259' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hacked phone belonging to a high-profile figure reveals alleged secret negotiations and large-scale financial transfers, underscoring how personal devices can leak sensitive diplomacy. The breach highlights security risks for top officials and the potential consequences of private data leaking into public view. It also raises questions about how such disclosures could reshape power dynamics and influence campaigns abroad.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称属于知名人物的被入侵手机曝光了秘密谈判和大额交易记录，凸显个人设备可能泄露敏感外交信息的风险。此事暴露出高层安全风险以及私人数据若外泄可能带来的后果。也引发对此类披露如何影响权力格局和国际博弈的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kadoa.com/energy-prices/insights/investor-owned-utilities' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Investor-owned utilities charge 23% more for electricity than public providers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>私营公用事业向用户收取的电价比公立提供者高出23%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Electricity prices charged by investor-owned utilities run about 23% higher than public providers. The gap highlights how privatization and regulatory structures influence consumer costs, even as reliability and service quality may vary. For tech and energy teams budgeting for grid usage and energy-intensive workloads, the differential matters for planning and resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>投资者所有的公用事业公司电价比公营提供者高出约23%。这一差距揭示了私有化与监管框架如何影响消费者成本，即便可靠性和服务质量也会有所不同。对于需要用电的技术团队和能源密集型工作负载而言，这一差异在预算、规划与韧性设计上具有显著影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kadoa-datasets.b-cdn.net/energy-prices/data/2026-10-23T14-44-17-949664b7/og-investor-owned-utilities.png' data-full='https://kadoa-datasets.b-cdn.net/energy-prices/data/2026-10-23T14-44-17-949664b7/og-investor-owned-utilities.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/10/20/accident-or-not-the-no-kings-takedown-is-why-you-shouldnt-rent-your-megaphone-from-zuck/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The No Kings Takedown Is Why You Shouldn&#x27;t Rent Your Megaphone from Zuck</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>No Kings Takedown：你不该把扩音器租给扎克伯格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 518</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TechDirt&#x27;s piece argues that relying on a single gatekeeper for reach—like Facebook&#x27;s megaphone—is risky. It uses the No Kings takedown to illustrate how platform power can backfire on users and why diverse channels matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TechDirt 讨论 No Kings takedown，强调别把话语权交给单一平台（如 Zuckerberg 的平台）可能带来的风险。该事件凸显平台垄断力量对创作者的潜在打压，强调多渠道传播的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='https://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50213925' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://block.xyz/dessa' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dessa: Conversation Model trained only on speech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dessa：仅用语音训练的对话模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50213925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 192</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An conversation model trained exclusively on spoken language data tests how far dialog capabilities can emerge without text-based training. The work highlights strengths in speech understanding and prosody handling, while revealing gaps in reasoning and abstract knowledge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>仅使用语音数据训练的对话模型用于测试在没有文本训练的情况下，对话能力能够达到何种水平。结果显示在语音理解和语调处理方面具备优势，同时在推理和抽象知识方面存在不足。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://block.xyz/dessa/_media/6865f35ec6013672f8f8/assets/marketing/variants/v3.jpg' data-full='https://block.xyz/dessa/_media/6865f35ec6013672f8f8/assets/marketing/variants/v3.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://po-ru.com/2026/10/18/why-im-no-longer-updating-this-repository' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I no longer care to work on any Ruby gems so I&#x27;ve archived all my repositories</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不再维护 Ruby gem，全部仓库已归档</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 514</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154518' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 508</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer announces archiving all Ruby gem repositories, effectively stepping back from maintenance. The move highlights open-source maintenance burnout and the fragility of ecosystems when maintainers disengage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位开发者宣布将归档所有 Ruby gem 仓库，等于退出维护。此举凸显开源维护的倦怠问题，以及在维护者退出时生态系统的脆弱性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keepandroidopen.org/?' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your Phone Is About to Stop Being Yours: 70 Days Until Lockdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的手机将不再完全属于你：距锁定还有70天</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 512</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 360</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A looming change threatens to drastically curb user control over smartphones, with only 70 days left before the change takes effect.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个即将到来的变化可能大幅削弱用户对智能手机的控制，距离生效仅剩70天。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://keepandroidopen.org/img/logos/fulu.org.png' data-full='https://keepandroidopen.org/img/logos/fulu.org.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50145337' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hybrid Reasoning Agents: LLM agents that think in latent space, act through text</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>混合推理代理：在潜在空间思考、通过文本执行行动的 LLM 代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 511</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers propose Hybrid Reasoning Agents that operate by thinking in a latent space while taking actions via text. The approach blends neural representations with text-based control to enable scalable, interpretable planning in complex tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者提出混合推理代理，其在潜在空间中进行推理，并通过文本执行行动。这种方法将神经表示与文本控制结合，提升在复杂任务中的可扩展性与可解释性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50198675' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/NVIDIA/OpenShell' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenShell – A safe, private runtime for fleets of autonomous AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenShell：为成群自主 AI 代理提供安全、私有的运行时</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50198675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 496</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50198675' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenShell provides a private, sandboxed runtime to run fleets of autonomous AI agents with strong isolation and governance. It aims to make enterprise deployments safer and easier by enforcing privacy-by-default and auditable workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenShell 提供私有、沙箱化的运行时环境，用于部署成群的自主 AI 代理，强调强隔离与治理。面向企业场景，聚焦隐私保护和可审计的工作流，提升安全性和合规性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fbaeb248dd5067686bb0eeefe867df5f2eed0feddc9e3d91f67e69de612ebee7/NVIDIA/OpenShell' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fbaeb248dd5067686bb0eeefe867df5f2eed0feddc9e3d91f67e69de612ebee7/NVIDIA/OpenShell' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50226419' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apricitas.io/p/ai-and-the-fall-of-the-creative-class' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America has lost 200k of jobs in media, film and the arts. How much is AI?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国在媒体、影视和艺术领域已失去20万份工作，AI 会带来多大影响？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50226419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 486</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50226419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece analyzes how AI could displace large numbers of creative workers, quantifying losses in media, film, and the arts. It frames AI as a productivity and transformation shock for the creative economy, with implications for wages, jobs, and policy responses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章量化了 AI 对创造性行业的潜在冲击，显示媒体、影视与艺术领域可能流失的工作量及趋势。讨论 AI 作为生产力与结构性变革的驱动力，对薪资、就业和政策提出了重要的现实考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!oz9x!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F999e3450-1df3-457d-8715-5e2e46d5121c_2886x1843.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!oz9x!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F999e3450-1df3-457d-8715-5e2e46d5121c_2886x1843.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stevekrouse.com/ends' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What shall I teach my son?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我应该教我的儿子什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 483</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 628</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal reflection on what to teach your child, urging emphasis on critical thinking, empathy, and problem-solving over rote metrics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇关于该教儿子什么的个人反思，强调批判性思维、同理心与解决问题能力的重要性，而非死记硬背的指标。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50160494' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openbsd.org/80.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenBSD 8.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenBSD 8.0 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160494' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 480</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160494' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 8.0 arrives as the newest stable release in the security-focused family, pushing forward on correctness and minimal attack surface. It brings base-system updates, improved hardware support, and ongoing hardening features that appeal to sysadmins who prioritize reliability and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 8.0 作为该安全优先发行线的最新稳定版本，继续强调正确性与较小的攻击面。该版本包含基线系统更新、对更多硬件的支持提升，以及持续的安全强化，适合追求高可靠性和稳健性的系统管理员。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openbsd.org/images/blowfishonbike-s.gif' data-full='https://www.openbsd.org/images/blowfishonbike-s.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/10/canadians-are-running-out-time-oppose-dangerous-c-22-surveillance-bill' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canadians are running out of time to oppose Bill C-22</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加拿大人已无时间反对 C-22 法案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 477</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A timely alert that Bill C-22 would expand surveillance powers, urging Canadians to mobilize before deadlines close.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>警示称 C-22 法案将扩大监控权限，敦促加拿大人在截止日期前行动，以应对对公民自由的潜在侵害。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50161346' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/icons-in-every-menu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No more icons in every menu by default</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>默认不再在每个菜单中显示图标</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50161346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50161346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A design shift disables default icons in menus to reduce visual clutter and improve focus. The change sparks debate on accessibility and discoverability, while leaving customization for power users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>设计转变在菜单中默认不再显示图标，旨在降低视觉杂乱并提升聚焦。此举引发对可访问性和可发现性的讨论，同时也让高级用户保留自定义选项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/macos-golden-gate-hig-icons.png' data-full='https://cdn.jim-nielsen.com/blog/2026/macos-golden-gate-hig-icons.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://steveblank.com/2026/10/24/europe-gets-a-wake-up-call/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Elon Musk Just Gave Europe a Reason to Replace American Technology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>埃隆·马斯克为何让欧洲有理由替代美国产技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50220913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 459</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50220913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 486</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing Europe now has more incentive to cultivate homegrown tech and reduce reliance on American platforms after moves by a high-profile U.S. tech leader. The piece suggests Europe could accelerate its digital sovereignty through supportive policies, standards, and data localization. It frames recent developments as a wake-up call for Europe to diversify its tech ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为，受一位知名美企领袖近期动作影响，欧洲在培育本土科技、减少对美国平台依赖方面获得更强的动机。作者指出欧洲可以通过友好政策、标准制定和数据本地化等举措加速数字主权建设，将其视为提升自身技术生态多样性的契机。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvczAud3AuY29tXC9pXC9ibGFuay5qcGciLCJ0eHQiOiJTdGV2ZSBCbGFuayIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiaGlnaHdheSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjo2NTk5NTg5fQ.-efmxdtN_YlMwMyz4RgR-akCDYfNNsBU6laamcldxGAMQ' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/_si/?t=eyJpbWciOiJodHRwczpcL1wvczAud3AuY29tXC9pXC9ibGFuay5qcGciLCJ0eHQiOiJTdGV2ZSBCbGFuayIsInRlbXBsYXRlIjoiaGlnaHdheSIsImZvbnQiOiIiLCJibG9nX2lkIjo2NTk5NTg5fQ.-efmxdtN_YlMwMyz4RgR-akCDYfNNsBU6laamcldxGAMQ' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mastodon.gamedev.place/@AshleyGullen/117473851565913919' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Facebook Pages require paid subscription to post more than two links per month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Facebook 页面：每月发布超过两个链接需付费订阅</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50167536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 456</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50167536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 395</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Facebook is gating link posting on Pages behind a paid subscription, limiting to two links per month. This marks a shift toward subscription-based reach for Page administrators. It raises concerns for marketers and developers who rely on organic link sharing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Facebook 对 Pages 的链接发布实施付费门槛，每月仅限发布两条链接，超过部分需订阅。此举体现了平台对可变盈利模式的推动，也可能影响依赖页面曝光的营销和开发者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.masto.host/mastodongamedevplace/media_attachments/files/117/473/851/121/967/109/original/2fb667edf9ac327a.png' data-full='https://cdn.masto.host/mastodongamedevplace/media_attachments/files/117/473/851/121/967/109/original/2fb667edf9ac327a.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OBS Studio 33 arrives with new features, performance tweaks, and bug fixes, continuing the project’s open-source momentum for live streaming and screen capture. The update improves stability and compatibility for creators relying on high-fidelity audio/video.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50202217' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/formal-verification' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs are making formal software verification feasible at a much larger scale</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大语言模型让形式化软件验证在更大规模上成为现实</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Large language models are making formal verification scalable beyond niche research by automating proof steps and suggesting invariants. This lowers the manual burden of proving software correctness and expands the range of verifiable systems—from compilers to safety-critical software. If proven robust, this approach could change how the industry builds trustworthy software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大语言模型正在让形式化软件验证的规模扩展到此前难以触及的范围。它通过自动化推理和证明生成，降低人工成本并帮助发现不变量。此举可能改变对软件正确性的信任方式，尤其在安全关键领域。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/80cf520cb540989c3643907f004b2514ec4d01f1-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/80cf520cb540989c3643907f004b2514ec4d01f1-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gridification（网格化）是什么？</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gridification refers to organizing systems or content into a grid-like structure to improve composability and scalability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gridification 指将系统或内容以网格状结构组织，以提升模块化和可扩展性。</p>
