<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wiz.io/blog/php2shell-php-rce' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PHP2Shell: AI uncovers critical RCE in PHP</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PHP2Shell：AI 发现 PHP 的关键远程代码执行漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 445</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180836' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-driven tool PHP2Shell uncovers a critical PHP RCE vulnerability, illustrating how AI can accelerate security research and the importance of secure coding practices. The finding underscores the need for rigorous input validation and timely patching of PHP deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 驱动的工具 PHP2Shell 发现了一个严重的 PHP 远程执行漏洞，展示了 AI 在安全研究中的潜力与风险。此发现强调对 PHP 部署进行严格的输入校验与快速修补的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50229587' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.panamericandispatch.org/p/meta-ignores-brazilian-court-order' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Ignores Brazilian Court Order to End Bolsonaro Favoritism</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 无视巴西法院命令，停止对博索纳罗的偏袒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50229587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 439</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50229587' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta ignored a Brazilian court order to end favoritism toward Bolsonaro on its platforms. The dispute highlights the tension between tech giants and regulators over political neutrality in Brazil, with possible sanctions for continued non-compliance. The case could shape how courts police platform behavior around elections in Latin America.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta据报无视巴西法院关于停止对博索纳罗偏袒的命令。此事凸显科技巨头与监管机构在巴西政治中立性上的博弈，若持续不合规可能面临制裁。此案或影响法院在选举期监管大型平台行为的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!CdTr!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F2d3b04ca-0141-4aca-81e2-f64fd77e8555_1157x626.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!CdTr!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F2d3b04ca-0141-4aca-81e2-f64fd77e8555_1157x626.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tvfandomlounge.com/disney-plus-price-hike-ads-premium-tier-nielsen-emmys-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Disney Raising Prices Days After Confirming Ads Will Now Show Up on Every Tier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>迪士尼在确认所有价位都将显示广告后提高价格</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 438</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 450</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Disney+ is raising prices shortly after confirming ads will appear at all tiers, signaling a tighter monetization strategy that blends ads with subscription value. The move could nudge subscribers toward ad-supported options or prompt churn among price-sensitive users, affecting the streaming market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在确认所有套餐都将包含广告后，迪士尼提高了订阅价格，反映出其对广告与订阅相结合的盈利策略。此举可能促使部分用户转向带广告的方案，或让价格敏感用户流失，影响流媒体市场格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cretezy.com/2026/ad-free-android-tv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An ad-free Android TV</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无广告的 Android TV</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50158942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 425</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50158942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project or article advocates for an ad-free Android TV experience, arguing it improves privacy, performance, and user focus. It discusses the tradeoffs of monetization, app discovery, and potential routes like custom ROMs or curated app ecosystems to remove ads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章倡议打造无广告的 Android TV 体验，主张提升隐私、性能与用户专注度。讨论了广告商业化、应用发现的权衡，以及通过定制 ROM 或精心管理的应用生态来实现无广告的可行路径。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50165953' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/typescript-rust-port/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tsrs: A Port of TypeScript to Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tsrs：TypeScript 到 Rust 的移植</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 425</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tsrs is a port of TypeScript tooling to Rust, enabling TypeScript-like workflows to run on a Rust-based runtime. The project promises safer memory guarantees and potential performance gains, inviting developers to re-evaluate toolchains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tsrs 将 TypeScript 的工具链移植到 Rust，使 TypeScript 工作流能够在 Rust 运行时上运行。该项目可能带来更安全的内存管理与潜在的性能提升，促使开发者重新评估工具链选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M5C82XAYSWX571T82A8AZRKS.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M5C82XAYSWX571T82A8AZRKS.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50162177' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/microg/GmsCore/wiki' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MicroG: Free-libre re-implementation of Google&#x27;s proprietary Android user space</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MicroG：对 Google Android 用户空间的自由开源实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50162177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 416</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50162177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>MicroG offers a libre reimplementation of Google Mobile Services, enabling Android runs without proprietary Google binaries. It supports many apps and services while prioritizing privacy, empowering custom ROMs and privacy-focused devices, though some apps may rely on proprietary features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MicroG 提供对 Google 移动服务的自由开源再实现，使用户在不使用官方谷歌二进制的情况下运行 Android。尽管兼容性可能有限，但它为隐私友好的设备与自定义 ROM 提供了可行选项，某些应用仍可能依赖专有特性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ac3cc3fa2e0b9af52f07bdef0024029835dfc06e1311dc682cb6f6212f67b610/microg/GmsCore' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/ac3cc3fa2e0b9af52f07bdef0024029835dfc06e1311dc682cb6f6212f67b610/microg/GmsCore' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50173716' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fulcrumsec.vg/pingcap/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PingCap TiDB Leaks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PingCap TiDB 漏洞曝光</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50173716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 412</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50173716' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers report TiDB leaks that could expose sensitive data or configuration details. The note outlines the scope, risk, and recommended mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究人员披露了 PingCap TiDB 的漏洞暴露风险，可能导致敏感数据或配置信息泄露。文章概述了受影响范围、风险等级及缓解措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fulcrumsec.vg/pingcap/pingcap_card.jpg' data-full='https://fulcrumsec.vg/pingcap/pingcap_card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50167028' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prestonbyrne.com/2026/10/20/ofcom-concedes-defeat/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ofcom concedes defeat in 4chan case</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ofcom 在 4chan 案件中认输</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50167028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 411</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50167028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 377</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In a high-profile regulatory dispute, Ofcom concedes defeat in a case involving 4chan, underscoring the limits of national regulators to police global platforms. The outcome may recalibrate expectations for cross-border moderation obligations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在一起备受关注的监管案件中，Ofcom 就 4chan 案件认输，凸显英国监管机构对全球性平台治理的有限能力。此结果可能重新校准对跨境内容治理的期待。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://prestonbyrne.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/hamster.jpg' data-full='https://prestonbyrne.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/hamster.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50141076' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron/wiki' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RepRapMicron</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RepRapMicron：开放源代码的微型3D打印平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 410</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141076' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RepRapMicron documents an open-source micro-scale motion platform for RepRap-style 3D systems, emphasizing sub-micrometer precision. The project offers mechanical designs, electronics, and firmware to enable precision micro-manufacturing at a hobbyist level. It showcases how open hardware can democratize advanced fabrication capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RepRapMicron 记录了一个用于 RepRap 系列的微型尺度运动平台，强调亚纳米级（亚微米）精度。该项目提供机械设计、电子和固件，面向爱好者层面的微型制造。它展示了开源硬件如何让高端加工能力走向大众。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/917851e063645a0adabf17ac1f2af1e51269f897b75681d18cce1c0f7e29cb1c/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/917851e063645a0adabf17ac1f2af1e51269f897b75681d18cce1c0f7e29cb1c/VikOlliver/RepRapMicron' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50185869' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://docs.gitlab.com/releases/patches/patch-release-gitlab-19-5-1-released/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitLab critical patch release</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitLab 发布关键补丁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50185869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 404</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50185869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitLab issued a critical patch release 19.5.1 addressing several high-severity vulnerabilities and performance issues. Users are urged to upgrade promptly, as attackers may exploit unpatched versions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitLab 发布了 19.5.1 版本的关键补丁，修复了若干高危漏洞并提升稳定性。强烈建议用户尽快升级，未打补丁的版本可能被利用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://docs.gitlab.com/gitlab-logo-footer.svg' data-full='https://docs.gitlab.com/gitlab-logo-footer.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50174687' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/security/2026/10/meta-muse-exposes-users-personal-and-business-secrets-when-they-share-conversations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse exposes users&#x27; personal and business secrets when they share conversations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse 在共享对话时暴露用户的个人与商业秘密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50174687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50174687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Muse reportedly exposes sensitive personal and business secrets when conversations are shared, highlighting a privacy risk in collaborative AI tools. The issue underscores the need for stronger data handling and access controls in shared contexts. Enterprises using such tools should re-evaluate data exposure surfaces and mitigate accordingly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Muse 在共享对话时暴露了用户的个人和商业秘密，凸显协作型 AI 工具中的隐私风险。问题强调需要在共享场景中加强数据处理与访问控制。使用此类工具的企业应重新评估数据暴露面并采取缓解措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/ai-robot-hand-caution-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/ai-robot-hand-caution-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50163961' data-tags='DevOps,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neowin.net/news/google-cloud-outage-takes-down-hsbc-revolut-bank-of-scotland-llyods-equifax-more/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Cloud outage affects almost all banking and other operations in Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google Cloud 故障影响欧洲几乎所有银行及其他业务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 396</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163961' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 203</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Google Cloud outage disrupted services across Europe, hitting banking operations and other critical apps. The incident underscores how deeply industries rely on major cloud providers and the potential domino effects of service outages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Cloud 故障波及欧洲多国，影响银行业务及其他关键应用。此事件凸显各行业对大型云服务商的高度依赖，以及服务中断可能带来的连锁影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50153664' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/world/ukraine/ukraine-first-drone-evacuation-wounded-soldier-frontlines-hexacopter-rcna604310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ukraine claims first drone evacuation of wounded soldier</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>乌克兰宣布首次用无人机从前线撤离受伤士兵</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50153664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 394</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50153664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 298</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ukraine reports the first drone-assisted evacuation of a wounded soldier from the front lines, signaling a potential new use case for unmanned aircraft in casualty extraction under combat conditions. While not replacing medevac helicopters, it could speed triage, reduce risk to human responders, and influence battlefield logistics and tech procurement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>乌克兰声称在前线首次实现受伤士兵的无人机撤离，标志着无人机在战场救援中的新应用。该做法并非取代直升机医撤，但可能加速分级救治、降低人类救援人员暴露风险，并影响未来的战场后勤与相关技术采购。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-10/261019-drone-rs-bd64e5.jpg' data-full='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-10/261019-drone-rs-bd64e5.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50214218' data-tags='Privacy,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/1017600/google-personal-intelligence-privacy-policy-ads-update' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Now Google says it might use your Gmail and Google Photos data to target ads</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌称可能把你的Gmail和Google照片数据用于定向广告</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50214218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50214218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is updating its privacy policy to allow using data from Gmail and Google Photos to target ads. That expands the data signals behind ad targeting beyond search activity and email, potentially improving relevance at the cost of privacy. The change underscores ongoing tensions between personalized advertising and user consent, making user controls more important.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌宣布隐私政策更新，可能将Gmail和Google照片中的数据用于定向广告。这一举动扩大了广告定向所依赖的数据来源，可能提升广告相关性，但增加隐私风险。此变动凸显个性化广告与用户同意之间的持续博弈，用户对控制权的需求将更加突出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/02/STK093_GOOGLE_D.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/02/STK093_GOOGLE_D.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.732984293194%2C100%2C78.534031413613&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50186010' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://google.github.io/styleguide/rustguide.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rust Style Guide</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌的 Rust 风格指南</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s Rust Style Guide codifies internal conventions for formatting, naming, error handling, and safety patterns, helping teams write consistent, secure Rust at scale. While tailored for Google, the guide offers useful patterns for the broader Rust community and OSS projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌的 Rust 风格指南将格式化、命名、错误处理与安全模式等内部约定系统化，帮助团队在大规模项目中保持一致、提高安全性。尽管面向谷歌内部，但该指南对整个 Rust 社区和开源项目也具备参考价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50166257' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kaba.cx/posts/20261020_Tor/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I got over 300 Tor exit nodes removed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何移除了超过 300 个 Tor 出口节点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 385</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer explains how they managed to have more than 300 Tor exit nodes removed from service endpoints, illustrating how abuse contacts and moderation policies intersect with anonymity networks. The piece offers practical insights into the ongoing arms race between access controls and Tor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者介绍了如何让超过 300 个 Tor 出口节点从服务端点下架，揭示滥用联系渠道与匿名网络治理的交叉点。内容展示了访问控制与 Tor 之间持续博弈的实用见解。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kaba.cx/img/tor/relays_offline_hero.png' data-full='https://kaba.cx/img/tor/relays_offline_hero.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.heistberlin.com/news/afd-leak-what-421-179-hacked-emails-show-about-the-german-far-right/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AfD Leak: What 421,179 Hacked Emails Show about the German Far Right</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AfD 泄露：421,179 封被盗邮件揭示德国极右翼的网络与协作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 549</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The leak includes correspondence from AfD members and affiliates, shedding light on fundraising schemes, alliances, and internal strategies. It raises questions about political influence networks online and the role of data breaches in exposing extremism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泄露邮件涉及 AfD 成员及关联方的通信，揭示筹资手段、联盟与内部策略等。信息披露点出极右翼网络的运作模式，以及数据泄露在揭露极端主义中的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/98/53/98532a3a-3542-4bcd-a05e-a2672d5c28c1/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/x-card--31--2.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/98/53/98532a3a-3542-4bcd-a05e-a2672d5c28c1/content/images/size/w1200/2026/10/x-card--31--2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50169053' data-tags='Open Source,Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/google-pytorch/torch_tpu' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch back end for TPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google 开源 TorchTPU：面向 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend for TPUs, making TPUs feel like a first-class citizen for PyTorch users. This lowers the barrier to running PyTorch models on Google&#x27;s accelerators and could shift performance comparisons across ML stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 将 TorchTPU 开源，提供对 TPUs 的原生 PyTorch 后端，使 PyTorch 用户能够直接在 TPUs 上运行模型。此举降低了在 TPU 上运行 PyTorch 的门槛，可能改变不同 ML 堆栈间的性能对比。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/32a1a94e5709ac3832cc25069cf7deaa4e3ef2a1f281b3be9fd463181d81fd11/google-pytorch/torch_tpu' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/32a1a94e5709ac3832cc25069cf7deaa4e3ef2a1f281b3be9fd463181d81fd11/google-pytorch/torch_tpu' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50217500' data-tags='Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://philipotoole.com/twelve-years-of-distributed-sqlite/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Twelve years of distributed SQLite</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>分布式 SQLite 的十二年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50217500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50217500' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A twelve-year look at distributed SQLite efforts shows how engineers tried to keep data in sync across devices without a central server. It weighs replication strategies, conflict resolution, and consistency models, and draws practical lessons for building offline-first apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对分布式 SQLite 的十二年尝试进行回顾，聚焦在没有中央服务器时如何在多设备之间保持数据同步。评估复制策略、冲突解决和一致性模型，并为构建离线优先应用提供实用经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/a1c9c248eeb5239c162a6a32c420209af702076cd0ed07d6ddbe00c269021a98?s=50&amp;d=identicon&amp;r=g' data-full='https://secure.gravatar.com/avatar/a1c9c248eeb5239c162a6a32c420209af702076cd0ed07d6ddbe00c269021a98?s=50&amp;d=identicon&amp;r=g' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50233779' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lynchlab.net/llm-skin-cancer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 10-year-old CNN outperforms frontier LLMs for skin cancer diagnosis</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>十年前的 CNN 在皮肤癌诊断中超越前沿 LLM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A decade-old CNN model reportedly outperforms frontier large language models on skin cancer diagnostic tasks, highlighting the strength of specialized vision systems for image-based medical problems. The result underscores that domain-specific models can outperform generic LLMs in certain tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款十年前的卷积神经网络在皮肤癌诊断任务中表现优于前沿的大型语言模型，凸显了专用视觉模型在图像类医疗问题上的优势。此结果提醒人们，在特定任务上，领域专用模型可能胜过通用的 LLM。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2gaqzonlm544h.cloudfront.net/skin-ai/samples/ISIC_7403025.jpg' data-full='https://d2gaqzonlm544h.cloudfront.net/skin-ai/samples/ISIC_7403025.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.southampton.ac.uk/~doug/quantum_physics/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I don&#x27;t understand quantum physics (2018)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不懂量子物理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219666' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A plain-language primer on quantum physics concepts such as superposition and entanglement, without heavy math. It emphasizes intuition and clear explanations, making the basics accessible to developers and curious readers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份简明指南用通俗语言介绍量子概念，不依赖高深数学。强调直觉与核心原理，适合对量子领域感兴趣的科技工作者与学生快速把握要点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50203161' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cs.utexas.edu/~EWD/transcriptions/EWD06xx/EWD667.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The foolishness of &quot;natural language programming&quot; (1978)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自然语言编程的愚蠢之处（1978）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50203161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50203161' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 260</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dijkstra argues that natural language programming is a misbegotten dream because natural language is ambiguous and ill-suited for precise instructions. The critique foreshadows why formal languages and rigorous semantics remain essential for reliable software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dijkstra 批评自然语言编程是一个错误的梦想，因自然语言容易产生歧义，难以给出精确定义。该论点预示了为何形式化语言和严格语义对可靠软件至关重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Broccoli2Shell discloses a Brotli decoder remote-code-execution vulnerability across Node.js, Python, and other ecosystems, prompting urgent patching and dependency hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Broccoli2Shell 指出在 Node.js、Python 等生态系统中的 Brotli 解码器存在远程代码执行漏洞，促请迅速打补丁并检查依赖。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gaming/2026/10/xbox-wants-you-to-pay-24-to-40-cents-per-hour-to-stream-games-you-already-bought/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xbox wants you to pay 24 to 40 cents per hour to stream games you bought</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xbox 希望你为云端串流付费，每小时约 0.24–0.40 美元</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xbox is considering charging about 0.24 to 0.40 USD per hour to stream games you already own, raising questions about ownership and the economics of game streaming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xbox 计划对云端串流收取每小时约 0.24–0.40 美元的费用，即便玩家已购买了游戏。这引发了对所有权与游戏串流商业模式的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pudding.cool/2026/10/kevin-bacon/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Real Kevin Bacon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>真实的凯文·培根</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-driven look at Kevin Bacon&#x27;s network centrality, offering fresh metrics and context to the classic Six Degrees idea. The story reframes celebrity connectivity beyond simple anecdotes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过数据可视化重新审视凯文·培根在网络中的影响力，提出新的衡量标准与背景信息，超越“六度分隔”的简单说法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50197404' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.taler.net/en/news/2026-13.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Getting ready to operate GNU Taler in Germany</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在德国部署 GNU Taler 的准备工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 359</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197404' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GNU Taler is being prepared for operation in Germany, signaling steps toward a privacy-preserving digital cash system in the EU. The update covers regulatory alignment, integration with local banks and PSPs, and how opt-in communities can participate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GNU Taler 在德国的落地正在推进，标志着在欧盟范围内推进隐私保护型数字现金系统的阶段性进展。更新聚焦监管合规、与本地银行及支付服务提供商的对接，以及社区参与的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.taler.net/images/logo-2021.svg' data-full='https://www.taler.net/images/logo-2021.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50153630' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://home.cern/cern-and-mistral-join-forces-to-accelerate-ai-for-science/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CERN and Mistral sign a statement of intent to establish a strategic partnership</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CERN 与 Mistral 签署建立战略伙伴关系的意向书</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50153630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50153630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CERN and Mistral sign a statement of intent to collaborate on AI for science, aiming to accelerate research with advanced AI models and shared compute resources. The partnership signals how AI could speed up data analysis in physics and related fields.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CERN 与 Mistral 签署战略伙伴关系意向书，计划在科学领域共同推动 AI 的应用，通过先进模型与共享计算资源加速研究。此举体现 AI 有望提升物理等学科的数据分析效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://home.cern/wp-content/themes/cern/public/images/logo-white.svg' data-full='https://home.cern/wp-content/themes/cern/public/images/logo-white.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50233929' data-tags='Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://talkweb.eu/openweb/4558/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I refuse to accept a censored Internet. Join Me at FOSDEM 27</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我拒绝接受被审查的互联网，加入我在 FOSDEM 27</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233929' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A manifesto-style appeal to defend an uncensored Internet, inviting readers to join a talk at FOSDEM 27. The message centers on preserving open standards and resisting censorship amid growing platform control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以宣言体撰写，倡导捍卫未被审查的互联网，并邀请读者参加 FOSDEM 27 的演讲。信息聚焦于保护开放标准、抗拒审查，以及在平台日益集中的环境中的自由与协作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://talkweb.eu/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/logo_bg.png' data-full='https://talkweb.eu/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/logo_bg.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50165801' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://peec.ai/blog/publish-stats-get-cited-the-tactic-that-put-my-data-all-over-the-web' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Publish fake stats, get cited in LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>发表伪造统计数据，易被大语言模型引用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 353</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165801' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tactic is described where fake statistics are published to be cited by large language models, illustrating how misinformation can leak into AI training data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中描述了一种策略：发布伪造的统计数据以被大型语言模型引用，揭示了错误信息如何进入 AI 训练数据并影响模型输出。文中讨论了来源可追溯性、防护措施，以及当虚假数据被当作事实时对模型可靠性的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://framerusercontent.com/images/g61nppL2S0T51TuBYG0bTxac2U.png?width=2150&amp;height=1126' data-full='https://framerusercontent.com/images/g61nppL2S0T51TuBYG0bTxac2U.png?width=2150&amp;height=1126' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50222133' data-tags='Web,Privacy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://freedns.afraid.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FreeDNS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FreeDNS：免费 DNS 托管服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50222133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 349</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50222133' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FreeDNS remains a long-running free DNS hosting option, offering dynamic DNS and subdomain management for hobby projects and small services. While it&#x27;s convenient, users should consider reliability, privacy, and potential ads when relying on free infrastructure for critical systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FreeDNS 继续提供免费的 DNS 托管、动态 DNS 及子域名管理，适合个人项目和小型服务使用。使用者应权衡可用性、隐私和对关键系统的依赖性，避免把核心服务寄托在免费基础设施上。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://freedns.afraid.org/images/bitcoin-logo.jpg' data-full='https://freedns.afraid.org/images/bitcoin-logo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50227356' data-tags='AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cerebras.ai/blog/how-we-deploy-trillion-parameter-frontier-models-on-cerebras' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cerebras Deploys Trillion-Parameter AI Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cerebras 部署万亿参数的 AI 模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50227356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 348</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50227356' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cerebras deploys trillion-parameter frontier models on its wafer-scale AI hardware. The feat demonstrates how specialized accelerators can handle models at scales once thought exclusive to huge clusters, potentially boosting inference throughput and reducing memory bottlenecks. It underscores a broader industry trend toward scale-optimized hardware for giant AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cerebras 在其晶圆级 AI 硬件上部署万亿参数的前沿模型。这一成就展示了专用加速器在处理大规模模型方面的潜力，或提升推理吞吐量并缓解内存瓶颈。再一次印证了面向“大模型”的专用硬件正推动 AI 规模扩张的行业趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/e4qjo92p/production/1796e3cccda1454e97d262833fb0bb0d6015032f-2786x1567.png?rect=0,53,2786,1463&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/e4qjo92p/production/1796e3cccda1454e97d262833fb0bb0d6015032f-2786x1567.png?rect=0,53,2786,1463&amp;w=1200&amp;h=630&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50154624' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kksimp/Mac.apk' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Android apps running native on macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Android 应用在 macOS 上原生运行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 191</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project demonstrates running Android apps natively on macOS, potentially removing emulation overhead. If mature, this could blur platform boundaries for developers and users, enabling smoother access to Android ecosystems on Apple Silicon.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目展示在 macOS 上原生运行 Android 应用，或许能消除仿真开销。若成熟，将模糊开发者与用户的跨平台边界，使 Android 生态在 Apple Silicon 上获得更顺畅的体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f5881ec2a90d87f6fd04da7dfe17cbec59e511b6b43b6f8ed683bc89213e4776/kksimp/Mac.apk' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f5881ec2a90d87f6fd04da7dfe17cbec59e511b6b43b6f8ed683bc89213e4776/kksimp/Mac.apk' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50150927' data-tags='Science,Health,Hardware,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/alternatives-to-animal-testing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alternatives to animal testing are finally taking off</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>替代动物测试正在显著推进</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 341</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advances such as organ-on-a-chip, sophisticated in vitro models, and in silico simulations are accelerating the replacement of animal testing. Regulatory acceptance is growing, reducing reliance on animal data in drug development and safety assessment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>器官芯片、先进体外模型以及计算模拟等技术正在加速替代动物测试的进展。监管认可度提升，药物开发与安全评估中对动物数据的依赖正在下降。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/a-photo-shows-a-hand-holding-a-small-clear-plastic-device-with-red-and-blue-lines-inside-it.jpg?id=67819184&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C834%2C0%2C834' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/a-photo-shows-a-hand-holding-a-small-clear-plastic-device-with-red-and-blue-lines-inside-it.jpg?id=67819184&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C834%2C0%2C834' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50186450' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/physicists-trap-a-single-antimatter-particle-for-a-month/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Physicists trap a single antimatter particle for a month</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>物理学家将单个反物质粒子困禁一个月</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 340</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>For the first time, researchers trapped a single antimatter particle for about a month, demonstrating long-term confinement and control. The milestone informs antimatter studies and could influence future antimatter storage and precision measurements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员将单个反物质粒子困禁约一个月，展现了对反物质的长期控制与稳定性。这一里程碑提升了对反物质的研究能力，或为未来的精密测量和储存方案提供线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/c8ce3ae2-d702-4225-a7d6-2c48e4cc1575/Particle-light-flare.jpg?m=1792593357.873&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/c8ce3ae2-d702-4225-a7d6-2c48e4cc1575/Particle-light-flare.jpg?m=1792593357.873&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50219700' data-tags='Hardware,AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(110) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Zagitta/gpu-unlock' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPU-unlock: FP/BF16 instruction issue rate on Nvidia 3000&#x27;s cards</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPU-unlock：NVIDIA 30系列显卡的 FP/BF16 指令发射率问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 336</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219700' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community project analyzes FP and BF16 instruction issue rates on Nvidia RTX 30-series cards, suggesting bottlenecks that affect mixed-precision workloads. The findings could inform driver optimizations, compiler choices, and how developers allocate precision in AI and HPC tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个社区项目对 NVIDIA RTX 30 系列显卡的 FP 与 BF16 指令发射率进行分析，揭示对混合精度任务的潜在瓶颈。相关结果可能为驱动优化、编译器选择以及开发者在 AI 和 HPC 任务中对精度的分配提供参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1a4a971a28614e008eb6bc0aa57f85fb8904b8f51c0e686a26eda25e0a2b68ba/Zagitta/gpu-unlock' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1a4a971a28614e008eb6bc0aa57f85fb8904b8f51c0e686a26eda25e0a2b68ba/Zagitta/gpu-unlock' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50234232' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theintercept.com/2026/10/01/oregon-police-misconduct-public-records-cost-fee/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oregon Police Are Charging a Local News Outlet $2.5M for Records About One Cop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>俄勒冈州警方就一名警察的记录向当地新闻机构索取250万美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Public records fees escalate when a local news outlet is charged $2.5 million for records about a single officer, raising concerns about transparency, access to information, and the use of exorbitant fees to suppress reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家本地新闻机构被要求就一名警察的记录支付250万美元的费用，此举引发对政府透明度、信息获取权及以高额费用压制报道的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Screenshot-2026-10-01-at-4.14.38-PM-e1790885738599.png?fit=1436%2C718&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' data-full='https://theintercept.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/Screenshot-2026-10-01-at-4.14.38-PM-e1790885738599.png?fit=1436%2C718&amp;w=1200&amp;h=800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50192900' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(112) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-111943' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CVE-2026-111943: Compromised Intermediate Certificate Authority</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CVE-2026-111943：中间证书颁发机构遭受妥协</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50192900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50192900' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compromised intermediate certificate authority threatens TLS trust across many deployments, as certificates it issued could be misused to impersonate sites. The incident underscores how a single compromised link in the certificate chain can erode global trust and trigger urgent revocation and re-issuance of affected certificates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CVE-2026-111943 指出中间证书颁发机构被攻破，攻击者可能滥用其签发的受信任证书伪装站点。此事件暴露证书链中单点受损就可能动摇全球信任，需要吊销、重新颁发并更新信任链。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50196837' data-tags='Energy,Business,DevOps,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techspot.com/news/114100-google-cleared-300-hectares-finnish-forest-data-center.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google cleared 300 hectares of Finnish forest</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌在芬兰清理了300公顷森林用于数据中心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50196837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50196837' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 245</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google reportedly cleared 300 hectares of Finnish forest for a data center expansion, drawing attention to land-use, environmental impact, and sustainability commitments in hyperscale infrastructure. The move underscores the tension between cloud capacity growth and conservation, putting pressure on regulators and suppliers to assess biodiversity and carbon footprints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，谷歌为扩建数据中心在芬兰清理了300公顷森林，引发对土地使用、环境影响及大规模基础设施的可持续性承诺的关注。此举凸显云计算容量扩张与自然保护之间的矛盾，促使监管机构和供应商重新评估生物多样性与碳足迹。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techspot.com/images2/news/bigimage/2026/10/2026-10-05-image-22.jpg' data-full='https://www.techspot.com/images2/news/bigimage/2026/10/2026-10-05-image-22.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50186129' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mistral.ai/news/mistral-large-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mistral Large 4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mistral Large 4</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 326</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186129' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI releases Mistral Large 4, the latest addition to its open-weight LLM lineup, touting improved instruction following and efficiency. The model strengthens the open-source AI ecosystem and intensifies competition with larger commercial rivals, potentially lowering costs for developers and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mistral AI 发布 Mistral Large 4，作为其开源权重大型语言模型阵容的最新成员，声称在指令跟随和效率方面有提升。该模型将进一步推动开源 AI 生态，并加剧与大型商业对手的竞争，为开发者和研究人员带来更具成本效益的选择。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sverigesradio.se/artikel/chat-gpt-och-claude-forbjuder-regeringskansliet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Swedish government offices to ban ChatGPT and Claude</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞典政府部门将禁用ChatGPT和Claude</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50207405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50207405' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Swedish government offices will ban ChatGPT and Claude amid concerns around data privacy and security. The move signals how public-sector AI adoption is balancing innovation with policy constraints, potentially delaying AI-assisted workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞典政府机构将禁止使用ChatGPT与Claude，因应对数据隐私与安全的担忧。此举反映公共部门在推动AI应用与遵守政策约束之间的权衡，或将影响AI辅助工作流程的推进速度。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/openai-models-google-cloud/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI models are coming to Google Cloud</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 模型将登陆 Google Cloud</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 318</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI models are coming to Google Cloud, letting enterprises run GPT-style models within GCP. The expansion broadens enterprise AI options while intensifying competition in the cloud AI space and raising data governance considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 的模型将进入 Google Cloud，企业可在 GCP 上直接使用类似 GPT 的模型。此举扩展了企业级 AI 的选择，并加剧云端 AI 领域的竞争，同时带来数据治理的新挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://imaginationlibrary.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dolly Parton&#x27;s Imagination Library</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多莉·帕頓的想象力圖書館</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dolly Parton&#x27;s Imagination Library is a long-running charity that mails free children&#x27;s books to families to boost early literacy. The program scales through partnerships and donations and has shipped millions of books to children, spanning multiple countries and communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多莉·帕頓的想象力圖書館是一项长期慈善计划，通过合作与捐赠为家庭寄送免费儿童读物，促进幼年读写能力。该项目在多国开展，向儿童提供了大量书籍，受到教育界关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://imaginationlibrary.com/ilData/usrFiles/2022/05/DPIL-Logo-195.png' data-full='https://imaginationlibrary.com/ilData/usrFiles/2022/05/DPIL-Logo-195.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50190455' data-tags='Science,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://web.maths.unsw.edu.au/~davidharvey/research/subnlogn.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The recently proposed o(n log n) integer multiplication algorithm [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>最近提出的 o(n log n) 的整数乘法算法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper proposes an integer multiplication algorithm with asymptotic time near n log n, challenging the long standing dominant methods. It outlines novel techniques in FFT-based arithmetic and modular transforms that push the theoretical boundary of large integer multiplication. Practical adoption remains uncertain due to big-constant factors and complexity of implementation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该论文提出一个在渐进时间复杂度接近 n log n 的整数乘法算法，试图挑战已有的 Schönhage-Strassen 等方法。文中描述了新颖的傅里叶变换相关技术与代数技巧，理论意义重大，可能改变大整数运算的基准。实际应用尚待验证，具体实现和常数因素将决定是否具备实用性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50206914' data-tags='Security,Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/jaredkushner/status/2113376217415958767' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dutch government had hacked into Jared Kushner&#x27;s phone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰政府曾入侵贾里德·库什纳的手机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50206914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50206914' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 202</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A revelation that a government agency accessed a high-profile target&#x27;s phone underscores state-level surveillance capabilities and digital forensics in political contexts. It spotlights how real-world security and privacy concerns intersect with policy and national-security reporting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>曝光显示荷兰政府曾入侵并获取了高知名度人物的手机数据，凸显国家级监控与数字取证在政治环境中的作用。事件暴露了现实世界的安全与隐私风险，以及相关的政策与国家安全议题间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1554293796438941697/FjT4jVh1_400x400.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1554293796438941697/FjT4jVh1_400x400.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50222622' data-tags='DevOps,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.roku.com/developer/gitlab-move' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Roku developer repositories moved from GitHub to GitLab</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Roku 将开发者代码库从 GitHub 转移至 GitLab</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50222622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50222622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 211</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Roku has moved its developer repositories from GitHub to GitLab, consolidating collaboration and CI/CD workflows on one platform. The shift will require contributors to update remotes and adapt to GitLab&#x27;s tooling, but it promises tighter security controls and potentially faster automation for builds and tests.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Roku 已将开发者代码库从 GitHub 转移至 GitLab，整合了协作与持续集成/交付工具。此次变更要求贡献者更新远程地址并调整工作流，但有望带来更严格的安全控制和更高效的自动化构建与测试。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.roku.com/assets/roku-logo-purple.png' data-full='https://www.roku.com/assets/roku-logo-purple.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50145507' data-tags='Privacy,Security,AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jrdsgl.com/if-you-dont-run-your-own-llm-proxy-how-the-fuck-do-you-even-know-whats-going-on/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you don&#x27;t run your own LLM proxy, how do you know whats going on?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你不运行自己的 LLM 代理，你如何知道发生了什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 298</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50145507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 213</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relying on hosted LLM APIs hides the model&#x27;s behavior and data flows; running your own proxy gives visibility into prompts, responses, and potential data leakage. It enables you to enforce policies, audit usage, and protect privacy, especially in regulated or sensitive environments. The piece argues that governance starts with visibility into the model&#x27;s operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>依赖云端 LLM API 让你看不到模型内部的行为和数据流动。自行搭建代理可让你监控输入输出、发现数据泄露风险，并执行策略与合规审查，尤其在受监管或敏感场景中尤为重要。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50146625' data-tags='Culture,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://colo.to/cyberspace.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>William Gibson and the trademarking of cyberspace</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>威廉·吉布森与网络空间的商标化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50146625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 283</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50146625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>William Gibson coined cyberspace in his cyberpunk fiction, helping define a cultural concept that later became a marketable asset. The article traces how the term migrated from literary prose to trademarked real estate, shaping IP strategy and branding in tech and policy. It highlights the friction between imaginative language and legal ownership in the digital era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>威廉·吉布森在科幻作品中首次提出“网络空间”这一概念，塑造了一个后来成为品牌资产的文化术语。文章追溯这一术语如何从文学创作转变为可注册商标，影响科技公司在品牌与法务上的策略。揭示了想象语言与数字时代法律产权之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://colo.to/pix/guell.jpg' data-full='https://colo.to/pix/guell.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 15.8.2 将 OpenGL 支持破坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141909' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS 15.8.2 breaks OpenGL support, causing rendering issues for apps that still rely on legacy GL contexts. Apple has deprecated OpenGL in recent updates, pushing developers toward Metal. The change underscores the risk of relying on deprecated APIs for future OS releases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 15.8.2 破坏了 OpenGL 支持，导致仍依赖旧版 GL 上下文的应用出现渲染问题。苹果已在更新中淡化 OpenGL，推崇 Metal，强调了依赖已弃用 API 的风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
