<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencealert.com/dogs-seem-to-agree-with-humans-that-bouba-is-round-and-kiki-is-spiky' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dogs seem to agree with humans that &#x27;bouba&#x27; is round and &#x27;kiki&#x27; is spiky</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>狗似乎认同人类的圆形-尖刺联想：Bouba/Kiki 实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dogs align with humans on Bouba/Kiki shape-label mappings, suggesting shared perceptual categories across species. The result supports the idea that some cross-species associations are rooted in perceptual priors rather than language.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>犬类在 Bouba/Kiki 的形状标签映射上与人类保持一致，表明不同物种可能共享感知类别。研究结果支持某些跨物种联想源自感知先验，而非语言。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/GettyImages-597067015-2560x1440-1.jpg' data-full='https://www.sciencealert.com/images/2026/10/GettyImages-597067015-2560x1440-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rtfeldman.com/fp-vs-c' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How close can pure functional programming get to C?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>纯函数式编程能有多接近 C？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50218195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50218195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pure functional programming excels at correctness, but performance parity with C remains a key question. The piece surveys language features, compiler optimizations, and benchmarks to show where FP can approach C in compute-heavy workloads and what design choices help close the gap.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纯函数式编程在正确性方面有优势，但在与 C 的性能对等方面仍存在差距。本文概览语言特性、编译器优化和基准测试，指出在高密度计算场景中纯函数式能接近 C 的程度，以及如何通过设计抹平差距。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50108717' data-tags='Programming,AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents/blob/main/benchmarking-new-models-on-slop-code-bench.md' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SlopCodeBench 10/2026 – Opus 5.5, Astra wins overall, xhigh considered harmful</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SlopCodeBench 2026年10月：Opus 5.5夺冠，Astra表现最佳，xhigh被认为有害</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50108717' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>In the SlopCodeBench 10/2026 report, Opus 5.5 leads the coding benchmarks while Astra posts the strongest overall score. The assessment also marks xhigh as harmful, highlighting ongoing safety and reliability debates for coding agents and the value of diverse benchmarking</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 SlopCodeBench 2026年10月的评测中，Opus 5.5领跑代码基准测试，Astra在总分上表现最优。同时将 xhigh 标记为有害，凸显编码代理在安全性与可靠性方面的持续讨论以及多维基准的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/06f7d77110a5abb10a459177df73d6d101245ed1db5aef47749201c99f4fbd0c/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/06f7d77110a5abb10a459177df73d6d101245ed1db5aef47749201c99f4fbd0c/humanlayer/advanced-context-engineering-for-coding-agents' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danielball.com/notes/ai_image_generation.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A practical guide to AI image generation, from diffusion to agents</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From diffusion models to agent-driven workflows, this guide demystifies building AI image generators. It covers practical pipelines, prompting strategies, safety considerations, and how autonomous agents can orchestrate multi-step image creation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从扩散模型到基于代理的工作流，本指南揭示了构建 AI 图像生成系统的实用流程。内容涵盖实际的工具链、提示策略、安全注意事项，以及让代理协同完成多步图像创作的思路。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50179828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50179828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post claims the Dutch National Cyber Security Center went offline because of PHP, fueling a broader debate about language choices for critical infrastructure. The claim highlights security and reliability questions around using PHP in national-grade systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有帖文称荷兰国家网络安全中心因使用 PHP 而离线，点燃了对关键基础设施语言选择的辩论。此说法突显在国家级系统中使用 PHP 所带来的安全性与可靠性疑问。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://krebsonsecurity.com/2026/10/feds-suspect-ransom-negotiator-ran-shinyhunters-extortion-site/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Feds suspect ransom negotiator ran ShinyHunters extortion site</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦调查人员怀疑勒索谈判者运营 ShinyHunters 的勒索网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50173487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50173487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US prosecutors allege a ransom negotiator ran the extortion site used by the ShinyHunters gang, acting as the broker between victims and attackers. If true, the case exposes a monetization choke point in cybercrime and could shape how authorities disrupt extortion ecosystems and pursue suspects. It underscores how sophisticated threat actors rely on intermediaries to pressure victims and extract payments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国检方指控一名勒索谈判人运营被 ShinyHunters 用来施压受害者的勒索网站，充当攻击者与受害者之间的中介。若指控成立，这一角色揭示了网络犯罪生态中的货币化环节，可能影响执法机构打击勒索网络的策略。事件凸显威胁行为体对中间人依赖以推动敲诈和索取赎金的作业流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://krebsonsecurity.com/b-doppel/17.png' data-full='https://krebsonsecurity.com/b-doppel/17.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon Threat Intelligence identified PHP2Shell exploitation linked to MadPot before public disclosure, underscoring the value of proactive threat intel in stopping campaigns early and guiding defensive updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊威胁情报在公开披露前就发现了与 MadPot 相关的 PHP2Shell 利用活动，凸显主动情报在提早遏制攻击与引导防御补丁中的价值。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://community.letsencrypt.org/t/lets-encrypt-is-adding-two-additional-remote-perspectives-for-domain-validation/252166' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt is adding two additional remote perspectives for domain validation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt 为域名验证增加两种远程验证视角</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt plans to add two additional remote perspectives for domain validation to improve robustness and accessibility. The move aims to diversify validation pathways, reduce reliance on a single validation method, and help users in constrained networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Let’s Encrypt 将为域名验证新增两种远程验证视角，以提升鲁棒性与可访问性。此举旨在丰富验证路径，降低对单一验证方式的依赖，并帮助在受限网络环境中完成验证。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/coordinated-disclosure-of-critical-vulnerabilities/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Patch the Planet: global response coordination as agents uncover vulnerabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Patch the Planet：代理发现漏洞时的全球响应协调</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 260</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181175' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 182</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global coordination is essential when agents uncover vulnerabilities, with a push for coordinated disclosure and cross-border collaboration. The framework outlines scalable remediation workflows and governance practices for responsible AI deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Patch the Planet 探讨当自动化代理发现漏洞时，全球各方如何协同应对，强调协同披露与跨境协作，以及大规模修复的治理方法。为负责任的 AI 部署提供了可操作的框架。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two PHP remote code execution CVEs with CVSS 10.0 are incoming, signaling an urgent patch priority for many PHP apps. Expect rapid advisories and widespread vendor updates as the community coordinates mitigations.</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加拿大议员提出限制赌博广告的措施，关注公众健康和消费者保护。提案可能重塑体育博彩与在线游戏的营销策略，迫使平台重新考虑定向投放、时机选择与信息披露。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50155245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50155245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercury 3 aims to scale diffusion-model workloads, delivering higher throughput and more efficient resource use for large-scale image generation. It reflects a broader push to production-grade diffusion at enterprises, with implications for cost, latency, and model deployment in AI pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercury 3 旨在实现扩散模型工作负载的大规模扩展，提升吞吐量并提高大规模图像生成的资源利用效率。此举反映了企业级生产化扩散模型的趋势，对成本、时延和AI流水线中的模型部署产生深远影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>偏远的西巴布亚发现被认为灭绝约 6000 年的小负鼠与飞鼠</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50162044' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 255</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50162044' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers rediscovered two marsupials in West Papua after thousands of years. The find highlights the enduring surprises in remote ecosystems and reinforces the case for protecting pristine habitats from development and habitat loss.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学家在西巴布亚偏远地区重新发现两种被认为已灭绝数千年的有袋动物。此发现凸显了偏远生态系统仍可能藏有新物种，也强调保护原始栖息地的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nypost.com/2026/10/24/business/google-illegally-tore-down-250k-trees-for-finland-data-center-infuriating-locals-report/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google illegally cut down 250K trees in Finland for data center</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌在芬兰非法砍伐250,000棵树建数据中心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50221676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50221676' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report claims Google cleared about 250,000 trees in Finland to build a data center, triggering environmental and local backlash. The controversy highlights the clash between large-scale infrastructure expansion and forest conservation in Europe.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称谷歌在芬兰为建数据中心非法砍伐约25万棵树，引发环保组织和当地社区的强烈反对。这一争议凸显在欧洲进行大规模基础设施扩张与森林保护之间的矛盾。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nypost.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/10/146289448.jpg?quality=75&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://nypost.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/10/146289448.jpg?quality=75&amp;strip=all&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/tech/1015844/airbnb-chores-house-rules-update' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Airbnb bans making guests do dishes and take out the trash</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Airbnb 禁止让房客洗碗和倒垃圾</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189150' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 230</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Airbnb updated policy to ban hosts from making guests do chores such as washing dishes or taking out trash. The change aims to improve guest experience and safety, though it may affect host expectations and cost management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Airbnb 更新政策，禁止房东要求房客做家务、洗碗或倒垃圾等。此举旨在提升体验与安全，但也可能改变房东的运营预期和清洁成本管理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/04/acastro_STK090_06.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.761835884455%2C100%2C78.47632823109&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://platform.theverge.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2025/04/acastro_STK090_06.jpg?quality=90&amp;strip=all&amp;crop=0%2C10.761835884455%2C100%2C78.47632823109&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/LKML-New-Trend-Vulnerabilities' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Several Linux kernel vulnerabilities accidentally posted to public mailing list</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多处 Linux 内核漏洞被误发至公开邮件列表</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 91</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Several Linux kernel vulnerabilities were accidentally published on a public mailing list, potentially leaking exploit details and increasing risk before patches reach users. This incident underscores the importance of secure disclosure practices and rapid remediation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多处 Linux 内核漏洞的细节被意外公开在公开邮件列表上，可能在补丁普及前被利用。此事件凸显负责任披露实践的重要性以及快速修复的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://projectdiscovery.io/research/how-abliterated-models-can-get-you-pwned' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How abliterated models can get you pwned</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>被抹除的模型如何让你中招</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 244</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers discuss how ablated or simplified models can be exploited to compromise systems, bypass safeguards, or leak data. The piece outlines attack vectors and practical mitigations for deploying ML models safely.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者讨论被抹除/简化的模型如何被利用，以绕过安全防护、窃取数据或造成系统被攻破。文中列出攻击向量及在部署 ML 模型时可采取的实际缓解措施。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50159616' data-tags='Security,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ipinfo.io/blog/france-fridges-hack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France&#x27;s supermarket fridges were vulnerable online for months</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国超市冷藏柜数月暴露在网上存在漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 242</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 221</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers found that connected fridge units in French supermarkets had misconfigurations or insecure access exposed online for months, enabling potential manipulation of inventory data or remote control. The incident underscores how consumer hardware in retail can be entry points for cyberattacks, and highlights need for better IoT management and firmware hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员发现法国多家超市的连网冷柜数月暴露在公开网络中，可能被未经授权的访问者远程操控或篡改库存数据。事件暴露零售业物联网设备的安全薄弱点，也凸显加强设备管理、固件更新和网络分段的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://website-cdn.assets.ipinfo.io/images/og/og_blog.png' data-full='https://website-cdn.assets.ipinfo.io/images/og/og_blog.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hirundo.io/blog/qwen3-coder-vulnerabilities' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen3-Coder writes less secure code when told it&#x27;s for a US persona</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>当被要求以美国人设输出时，Qwen3-Coder 代码更不安全</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50179706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 241</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50179706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 117</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3-Coder outputs less secure code when asked to adopt a US persona, revealing how persona constraints can impact security; emphasizes need for safeguards and cross-checks in AI coding tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>当被要求按美国人设输出代码时，Qwen3-Coder 的代码安全性下降，揭示角色设定可能对安全性造成负面影响。强调需要对 AI 编码工具加入保护措施与交叉检查。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/660fce732403d63a75c55289/6ad7819dd5fa2641cf6f79fa_qwen3-coder-01-gap-closed.png' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/660fce732403d63a75c55289/6ad7819dd5fa2641cf6f79fa_qwen3-coder-01-gap-closed.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50139067' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://t3.at/blog/2026-10-17-parser-combinators/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parser Combinators</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解析器组合子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parser combinators are a functional-programming technique for building parsers by composing small parsing functions. The piece surveys core ideas, common combinators, and practical patterns, offering examples across languages and showing how to get readable, maintainable parsers without writing a monolithic parser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解析器组合子是通过将小型解析函数组合来构建解析器的函数式编程技术。文中概述了核心思想、常用组合子与实践模式，并给出跨语言示例，展示如何用模块化方式实现可维护的解析器。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://t3.at/assets/series/parser-combinators.jpg' data-full='https://t3.at/assets/series/parser-combinators.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/openai/openai-cli' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI CLI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 命令行界面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50191507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50191507' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI released an official command-line interface, letting developers interact with models, manage API keys, and automate tasks from the terminal. The CLI lowers barriers for scripting, automation, and CI workflows, complementing the Python client.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布官方命令行界面，方便开发者在终端与模型交互、管理 API Key 以及执行自动化任务。CLI 降低了上手门槛，适合在脚本、持续集成等场景使用，与 Python 客户端形成互补。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8bfbc5c47cab19c9da563b52791fbe94f4ba600d7274bb4ec3d12fcc114014b8/openai/openai-cli' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8bfbc5c47cab19c9da563b52791fbe94f4ba600d7274bb4ec3d12fcc114014b8/openai/openai-cli' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.albertodenia.com/writing/code-is-clay-now' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Code is clay now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代码如今如黏土</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50153531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 237</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50153531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues code is malleable like clay, shaped by tools, languages, and AI-driven assistants. It reflects on how the craft of programming evolves as developers mold and remix components.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章把代码比作黏土，强调工具、语言与 AI 助手共同塑形代码的能力。它反映了开发者在设计、组合和重塑组件时，编程实践的演变。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.albertodenia.com/_app/immutable/assets/code-is-clay-now-hero.D0t9h_rd.jpeg' data-full='https://www.albertodenia.com/_app/immutable/assets/code-is-clay-now-hero.D0t9h_rd.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50156206' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://peter.eisentraut.org/blog/2026/10/09/the-reverts-will-continue-until-morale-improves' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The reverts will continue until morale improves</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回滚将继续，直至士气改善</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50156206' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50156206' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues that frequent code reversions are symptomatic of governance and morale issues in the development team. It suggests balancing stability with progress, noting that ongoing reverts can undermine confidence and slow feature delivery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>此文将频繁回滚视为治理与士气问题的信号，强调在稳定与进展之间寻求平衡。持续回滚可能削弱团队信心并拖慢新功能交付。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50136126' data-tags='AI,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=cNm4zyUtWj0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RuneScape Bench: MMOs for Agents [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RuneScape 基准测试：面向代理的 MMO [视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50136126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RuneScape Bench tests AI agents in MMOs, using the game as a sandbox to study agent behavior and learning in interactive environments. The takeaway is that game worlds remain valuable for benchmarking AI that must reason under uncertainty and real-time constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RuneScape 基准测试在 MMO 中评估 AI 代理，将游戏世界作为交互环境的试验场。结论是游戏环境仍然是评估在不确定性与实时约束下推理能力的 AI 的宝贵基准。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50199389' data-tags='Gaming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/maxilevi/project-hedra/tree/pre-ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hedra is a 3D open world game I wrote from scratch while in highschool</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hedra：高中时从零写成的 3D 开放世界游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50199389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50199389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hedra is a 3D open world game created from scratch in high school. This solo project demonstrates independent game development skills, including world-building, controls, and basic engine work, offering a peek into a teen&#x27;s indie effort.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hedra 是作者在高中时独自从零开始开发的 3D 开放世界游戏。该作品展示了单人开发的完整流程，包括世界构建、控制与基础引擎工作，呈现青少年独立游戏开发的有趣案例。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blogs.lse.ac.uk/impactofsocialsciences/2026/10/19/academics-have-a-duty-to-defend-open-knowledge-systems-like-wikipedia/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Academics have a duty to defend open knowledge systems like Wikipedia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>学者有义务捍卫开放知识体系，如维基百科</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50170917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50170917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece argues academics bear responsibility to defend open knowledge ecosystems; discusses openness vs. enclosure debates and suggests actions for scholars to support open knowledge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观点文章认为学者有责任捍卫开放知识体系，如维基百科，讨论开放与封闭之争，并提出支持开放知识的行动方向。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50223647' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29162' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why is AI generated language so hard to understand?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么AI生成的语言这么难以理解？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50223647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 229</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50223647' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 186</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper argues that AI-generated text can be difficult to understand due to misalignment between training goals and human intent, variability in prompts, and emergent model behavior. These factors complicate evaluation, reliability, and safety when deploying language models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文认为，AI 生成的文本难以理解，原因包括训练目标与人类意图之间的错位、提示的变异性以及模型行为的涌现性。这些因素使在部署语言模型时的评估、可靠性和安全性更加复杂。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50230181' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(160) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/mike64_t/status/2114150399406202896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Samsung running a TensorFlow lite model on 960x540 image to track you</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三星在960x540图像上运行 TensorFlow Lite 模型以跟踪用户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50230181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50230181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Samsung deploys an on-device TensorFlow Lite model that analyzes 960x540 video frames to track user activity. While processing happens locally, the approach raises privacy questions about what is inferred, stored, or shared from on-device analytics. It signals a broader push toward edge ML in consumer devices, with privacy-by-design as a key challenge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三星在960x540分辨率的图像上运行 TensorFlow Lite 模型以分析用户行为。尽管推理在本地完成，但这引发关于在本地分析数据会不会被进一步推断、存储或分享的隐私担忧。这体现了边缘 ML 在消费级设备上的普及趋势，隐私设计仍是关键挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HVb1282bgAAg6jY?format=webp&amp;name=large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HVb1282bgAAg6jY?format=webp&amp;name=large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>也许真的存在蛋价卡特尔</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50152970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50152970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Politico examines whether egg price volatility points to cartel-like conduct in a staple market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《政治家》刊文探讨蛋价波动是否暴露出基础食品市场的卡特尔行为。文章分析市场力量与供应动态如何影响价格可负担性，以及监测价格所依赖的数据信号。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50139692' data-tags='Legal,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.niemanlab.org/2026/10/legal-experts-warn-metas-new-terms-still-grant-broad-scraping-permissions-despite-language-changes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Legal experts warn Meta&#x27;s new terms still grant broad scraping permissions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法律专家警告：Meta新条款仍授予广泛抓取权限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 223</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50139692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 62</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Legal scholars warn that Meta&#x27;s updated terms continue to grant broad rights to scrape data, despite language changes. The stance could enable large-scale data harvesting, raising concerns for researchers, startups and privacy advocates about how the platform&#x27;s data can be used.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法律专家警告，尽管措辞有所改动，Meta更新条款仍授权广泛抓取数据。这可能允许大规模数据收集，引发研究者、初创公司和隐私倡导者对平台数据使用的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/AdobeStock_1108807119_Editorial_Use_Only-700x440.jpeg' data-full='https://www.niemanlab.org/images/AdobeStock_1108807119_Editorial_Use_Only-700x440.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50193170' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://globus.software/blog/2026/10/16/cutting-map-polygons/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Faster than earcut: triangulating 3.7M map polygons in 489 ms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>比 earcut 快得多：在 489 ms 内对 370万地图多边形进行三角剖分</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193170' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193170' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An optimization beats earcut by large margins, triangulating 3.7 million map polygons in 489 ms. This showcases aggressive, low-level geometry processing improvements that can dramatically speed up real-time map rendering and GIS workloads. The result hints at practical gains for interactive maps, navigation, and spatial data pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种优化在三角剖分地图多边形上取得巨大提升，370万的多边形在489毫秒内完成。此举展示了对几何处理的底层优化如何显著提升实时地图渲染和地理信息系统工作负载的速度，预计对交互式地图、导航和空间数据管线具有实际意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://globus.software/img/social-card.webp' data-full='https://globus.software/img/social-card.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A centralized tracker for CVE disclosures, helping security teams monitor vulnerability severity, affected products, and disclosure timelines. It supports prioritization and remediation planning across complex software stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个集中化的 CVE 漏洞信息跟踪工具，帮助安全团队监控漏洞的严重程度、影响产品和披露时间表。便于在复杂的软件堆栈中进行修复优先级排序与整改计划。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.picheta.me/post/killswitch-lang/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built a programming language that LLMs can&#x27;t use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我设计了一个连 LLM 都用不了的编程语言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50135762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50135762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I built a programming language that LLMs can&#x27;t use, exploring design choices that frustrate model-assisted code generation. The result, Killswitch Lang, highlights challenges in tooling, language features, and safeguards for human-centric programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我设计了一个让大型语言模型无法使用的编程语言，探讨了阻碍模型辅助代码生成的设计选择。其成果强调了对编程工具、语言特性以及人为驱动开发的安全性挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.computerworld.dk/art/297226/digitaliseringsministeriet-har-i-al-stilhed-droppet-stort-anlagt-forsoeg-med-open-source-alternativ-til-microsoft' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Danish Government silently drops plan to dump MS Office</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>丹麦政府悄然放弃用开源替代 MS Office 的计划</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Danish government quietly abandoned its plan to replace Microsoft Office with open-source alternatives. The move highlights the practical challenges of large-scale government IT migrations, including cost, user training, and vendor ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>丹麦政府悄悄放弃用开源替代 MS Office 的计划。此举凸显在公部门实施大规模软件迁移的成本、培训和供应商生态挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50157664' data-tags='Politics,Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(168) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hexane.cc/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Germany&#x27;s far-right AfD: half a million internal documents leaked</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国极右 AfD 内部约50万份文件泄露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50157664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50157664' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Half a million internal AfD documents were leaked, revealing details of the party&#x27;s internal organization, operations, and data practices. The leak could affect fundraising oversight, internal power dynamics, and how the party targets voters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>大约50万份 AfD 内部文件被泄露，揭示该党的内部组织、运作和数据实践细节。此次泄露可能影响资金监管、内部权力结构，以及对选民动员的策略。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50210067' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bugzilla.mozilla.org/show_bug.cgi?id=2083449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>eMudhra ignored Mozilla Root investigation deadline; blames social media</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>eMudhra 未在 Mozilla 根证书调查期限内完成回应；称把责任推给社媒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50210067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50210067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla&#x27;s Root Store investigation requires CA responses by a deadline; eMudhra reportedly missed it and publicly blamed social media for the confusion. The episode underscores the fragility of the certificate trust system and the importance of timely, transparent communication for browser trust. It may trigger additional scrutiny or actions from Mozilla.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 的根证书调查要求 CA 在截止日期前提交回应，eMudhra 据称未在期限内回应，并公开将问题归咎于社交媒体传播的信息。此事暴露了证书信任体系的脆弱性，也凸显了在维护浏览器信任过程中及时、透明沟通的重要性，可能引发进一步审查。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bugzilla.mozilla.org/static/v20261021.1/extensions/BMO/web/images/moz-fav-one-color-white-rgb.svg' data-full='https://bugzilla.mozilla.org/static/v20261021.1/extensions/BMO/web/images/moz-fav-one-color-white-rgb.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.boredhackerblog.info/2026/10/plex-vulnerabilities-being-exploited-in.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plex vulnerabilities being exploited in the wild to drop a cryptominer</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50152321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50152321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plex vulnerabilities are being exploited in the wild to drop a cryptominer, highlighting active threat activity in consumer media server software and the need to patch promptly.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Plex 漏洞在实际环境中被利用以部署加密货币矿工，凸显消费者媒体服务器软件的实时威胁活动与及时打补丁的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open MicroManipulator – A sub-micrometer 3D motion control plattform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Open MicroManipulator：亚微米级三维运动控制平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140001' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open MicroManipulator is a sub-micrometer 3D motion control platform with open hardware and firmware. The project aims to democratize precision manipulation for research and education, enabling ultra-fine control in labs and makerspaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Open MicroManipulator 提供一个开源硬件与固件的亚微米级三维运动控制平台，旨在让研究与教育领域的高精度操作民主化，便于实验室和创客空间实现极细微的控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/744c82ff1502a583a645f6d75adf9c53ccfd16dc173877c7950768d0798069df/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/744c82ff1502a583a645f6d75adf9c53ccfd16dc173877c7950768d0798069df/0x23/MicroManipulatorStepper' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise essay argues that well-made, resilient ideas endure against the odds, offering practical lessons for design and engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇简短的文章论证，精心打造、具备韧性的“好类型”在重重困难中依然能坚持，为设计与工程提供切实的教训。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50186215' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nmdoj.gov/press-release/the-new-mexico-department-of-justice-secures-another-landmark-victory-against-big-tech-with-historic-25-billion-penalty-against-facebook/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta hit with $25B penalty for deceiving users about privacy on Facebook</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta因在Facebook隐私方面欺骗用户被罚160亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 207</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186215' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The New Mexico DOJ imposes a $25 billion penalty on Meta for deceptive privacy practices on Facebook, marking a major privacy-enforcement milestone. The decision signals intensified scrutiny over how social platforms disclose data handling and may raise compliance costs for big tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新墨西哥州司法部对 Meta 的处罚金额高达250亿美元，指控其在 Facebook 的隐私披露方面存在误导行为，成为隐私执法的重要里程碑。该决定凸显对社交平台数据处理披露的加强监管，可能增加大型科技公司的合规成本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nmdoj.gov/wp-content/uploads/NMDOJ-Logo-Primary-Horizontal-white-gold-Long.png' data-full='https://nmdoj.gov/wp-content/uploads/NMDOJ-Logo-Primary-Horizontal-white-gold-Long.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tyma.com/my-cringe-story/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>My Friend Bob Cringely</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我的朋友 Bob Cringely</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166794' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cringe-filled personal tale involving tech scribe Bob Cringely offers a candid look at industry hype, credibility, and how to navigate past personas in tech culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段关于科技作家 Bob Cringely 的尴尬往事，揭示科技圈的 hype 与可信度问题，以及在行业朋友圈中识别人物的经验教训。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50201445' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/WHEQps5PK8LrrcTAb/how-does-fable-5-1-hide-messages-in-images' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How does Fable 5.1 hide messages in images?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fable 5.1 如何在图像中隐藏信息？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50201445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50201445' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at the steganographic techniques used by Fable 5.1 to conceal data inside images. It covers payload capacity, detectability, and potential misuse, highlighting trade-offs for secure messaging and watermarking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Fable 5.1 在图像中隐藏信息的隐写技术进行了分解，揭示了载荷容量、可检测性和潜在滥用的权衡。此分析强调了在实现安全信息传递和水印时需权衡的实际考量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1/mirroredImages/WHEQps5PK8LrrcTAb/h9vfucmjzf2tlo8t4ayf' data-full='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1/mirroredImages/WHEQps5PK8LrrcTAb/h9vfucmjzf2tlo8t4ayf' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50176082' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rumple.github.io/blog/2026/10/20/asking-llm-for-confidence.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using LLM for confidence scoring is a bad idea</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用大模型进行置信度评分是个坏主意</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50176082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50176082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that using LLMs to generate confidence scores is a bad idea because such scores are poorly calibrated and can mislead users. It recommends separate uncertainty estimates, calibration, or human in the loop instead of relying on model self assessments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇博文质疑让大型语言模型输出自我置信度的做法，指出此类分数往往标定不准且容易产生误导。建议使用独立的不确定性指标、信息检索校验或人工在环等替代方法，而非依赖模型自我评估的置信度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50230384' data-tags='Open Source,Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ihhub/fheroes2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>fheroes2: A recreation of Heroes of Might and Magic II game engine</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>fheroes2：Heroes of Might and Magic II 引擎的再现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50230384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50230384' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>fheroes2 recreates the Heroes of Might and Magic II engine as an open-source project, enabling cross-platform play with faithful restoration and community-driven improvements. It highlights how classic strategy games can find new life through preservation and collaborative development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>fheroes2 将《英雄无敌2》引擎以开源方式重新实现，支持跨平台运行并忠实还原原作，同时允许社区共同改进。此举展示了通过保留与协作开发，经典策略游戏能够焕发新生。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/205404938/bc5eed00-069a-11eb-80e0-094a5698cab5' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/205404938/bc5eed00-069a-11eb-80e0-094a5698cab5' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50144066' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.getlazarus.org/dynamic/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Free Pascal compiler now supports dynamic packages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Free Pascal 编译器现已支持动态包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 200</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144066' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Free Pascal compiler now supports dynamic packages, enabling runtime-loading of modular components and plugins. This makes it easier to distribute extensible Pascal apps and reduces binary bloat by loading code on demand, aligning with Lazarus/LF project workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Free Pascal 编译器现已支持动态包，允许在运行时加载模块和插件，提升可扩展性并降低初始二进制体积。此举与 Lazarus、跨平台 Pascal 应用的生态更紧密结合。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50144005' data-tags='Politics,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hoaspy.com/hoa/fl/florida-hoa-housing-crisis.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Florida&#x27;s HOAs and condo associations are making the housing crisis worse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>佛罗里达州的 HOA 与公寓协会正在加剧住房危机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50144005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 398</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Florida HOAs and condo associations constrain housing supply via rules and fees, worsening the state&#x27;s housing affordability crisis. The piece argues for policy reforms and smarter, scalable housing development approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>佛罗里达州的业主协会和公寓协会通过规则和费用限制住房供应，进一步加剧了该州的可负担性危机。文章主张进行政策改革与更高效、可扩展的住房开发。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/10/16/us/math-ai-terence-tao.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;I&#x27;m on a war footing&quot;: Terry Tao and the crisis in math</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“我正处于战争准备状态”：泰勒·陶与数学危机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 197</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50118165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Terry Tao argues that the mathematics community faces a crisis driven by AI, complexity, and funding pressures, calling for stronger collaboration and renewed focus on foundational problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>泰勒·陶指出，受人工智能、研究经费和学科挑战等因素影响，数学界正面临危机。他呼吁加强合作与重新聚焦基础问题，强调 AI 对研究文化与培养年轻数学家的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dsmurrell/onkey' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: OnKey – I&#x27;m a bad singer, so I built an app to show where I&#x27;m off-key</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OnKey：我走音，于是做了个应用标出走音点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195488' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 83</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OnKey analyzes your singing pitch in real time and marks off-key moments, turning practice into actionable feedback. Built as a Show HN project, it demonstrates how accessible audio analysis tools can be built with open-source tech and signal processing. It could inspire more consumer-friendly music-improvement apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OnKey 实时分析你的歌声音高并标出走音片段，把练习变成可执行的反馈。作为 Show HN 项目，展示了用开源技术和信号处理构建音乐分析工具的可行性。它或许会激发更多面向大众的发声训练应用的开发。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入探讨消防逃生梯的现状，分析在安全性、老化改造和城市设计方面的挑战，以及为何一些城市对外部逃生梯的依赖在减少，对建筑法规和居住安全意味着什么。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50195002' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The alien space of science: Coherent but cognitively unavailable directions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学的外星空间：自洽但认知不可达的方向</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 191</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195002' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 79</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Some scientific directions are internally coherent yet cognitively inaccessible to humans, challenging our intuition about progress. It invites reflection on how we evaluate and supervise such explorations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该论断指出，某些科学方向在理论上自洽，但对人类来说却难以理解。它探讨了对 AI 辅助发现的影响，以及机器可能走出人类推理范围的情境。提醒我们在评估与监督探索过程时，需要新的标准与方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://entropicthoughts.com/nixos-is-making-me-less-afraid' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NixOS is making me less afraid</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NixOS 让我不再那么害怕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A veteran user shares how NixOS improves confidence in system management through reproducible configurations and safe rollbacks, reducing fear of drift and dependency hell. The post argues for declarative, hermetic system design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位用户分享 NixOS 如何通过可重复的配置和安全回滚，提升对系统管理的信心，减轻系统漂移和依赖地狱的担忧。文中倡导声明式、封闭式的系统设计。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50205816' data-tags='Open Source,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ClickHouse/ClickBench/pull/2165' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Remove pgrust: the entry is cheating (per-query kernels and result caching)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>移除 pgrust：条目被认定为作弊（逐查询内核与结果缓存）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205816' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 148</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The project proposes removing pgrust after indications of cheating, including per-query kernels and result caching that skew benchmarks. The move reinforces the need for honest, transparent benchmarking in open-source projects and could affect credibility of reported performance figures. Maintainers emphasize fair competition and reproducibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在发现存在作弊迹象（如逐查询内核与结果缓存扭曲基准结果）后，项目组提议移除 pgrust。此举强调在开源基准测试中保持公正、透明的重要性，可能影响对性能数据的信任度，并敦促维护者关注可复现性与公平竞争。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4870be30cb291ef6f3ef20ef4fd4192b92a854ec1ef7d9bed8b58b200e3d9a47/ClickHouse/ClickBench/pull/2165' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/4870be30cb291ef6f3ef20ef4fd4192b92a854ec1ef7d9bed8b58b200e3d9a47/ClickHouse/ClickBench/pull/2165' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.judgments.fedcourt.gov.au/judgments/Judgments/fca/single/2026/2026fca1552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Epic Games Inc v Apple Inc</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Epic Games Inc.诉 Apple Inc.</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190168' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 190</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50190168' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Epic Games v Apple case in Australia centers on app store competition, platform fees, and digital marketplaces. A recent judgment could influence how developers access distribution and how regulators view gatekeeping power on mobile ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在澳大利亚的 Epic Games 对 Apple 的案件聚焦应用商店竞争、平台费与数字市场的公平性。相关判决可能影响开发者的分发渠道以及监管机构对移动生态系统垄断行为的评价。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50166502' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yoyo-code.com/2026-is-exciting-year-for-rust-compiler-and-me/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>2026 is an exciting year for the Rust compiler (and me)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 年将是 Rust 编译器的重要一年（也让作者兴奋）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50166502' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>2026 is shaping up as an exciting year for the Rust compiler, with anticipated improvements to const generics, compiler performance, and tooling. The author shares enthusiasm about the trajectory and possible impact on systems programming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 年将成为 Rust 编译器的重要一年，预计在常量泛型、编译器性能和工具链等方面带来改进。作者对这一发展轨迹及其对系统编程的潜在影响表示兴奋。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mastodon.catgirl.cloud/@49016/117475976434068995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A complete bypass of eMudhra’s 1FA OTP login system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对 eMudhra 1FA OTP 登录系统的彻底绕过</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50199757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50199757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 66</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers demonstrate a complete bypass of eMudhra’s 1FA OTP login, exposing critical weaknesses in OTP-based authentication used for identity verification. The finding underscores the fragility of one-time-password schemes and may drive demand for more robust multi-factor authentication.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者展示了对 eMudhra 的 1FA OTP 登录系统的彻底绕过，暴露了用于身份验证的一次性验证码的关键缺陷。这一发现凸显了单次性密码认证的脆弱性，可能推动对更强大多因素认证的需求。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 is released with security fixes and new features, signaling ongoing evolution of the crypto stack. The update may deprecate some APIs and require developers to test for compatibility with existing software. It’s a reminder to keep cryptographic dependencies up to date in production systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenSSL 4.1.0 发布，包含安全修复与新特性，体现密码学库的持续演进。此次更新可能对某些 API 进行弃用并要求开发者进行兼容性测试，提醒在生产环境中保持加密依赖的最新状态。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kottke.org/26/10/how-japanese-dried-bonito-is-made' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Japanese Dried Bonito Is Made</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本干鲣鱼片是怎么做成的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 184</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50193936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains the traditional process for making katsuobushi (dried bonito): boil, smoke, dry, and age the skipjack tuna with mold before shaving into thin flakes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章介绍日本干鲣鱼片的传统制作流程：先煮后烟熏、多轮晾干并经霉菌发酵，最终压制成坚块再刨成薄片。此过程强调风味与保存的平衡，以及高品质出汁所需的耐心工艺。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.ytimg.com/vi/HsMbNVEJp9g/sddefault.jpg' data-full='https://i.ytimg.com/vi/HsMbNVEJp9g/sddefault.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50202762' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jank-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jank – The native Clojure dialect with seamless C++ interop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jank——拥有无缝 C++ 互操作性的原生 Clojure 方言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202762' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jank is a native Clojure dialect designed for seamless interop with C++, enabling Lisp-like code to call into native libraries efficiently. It blends Lisp productivity with C++ performance, appealing to performance-critical and polyglot projects. Adoption is experimental and mostly exploratory at this stage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jank 是一种原生 Clojure 方言，旨在实现与 C++ 的无缝互操作，使 Lisp 风格的代码能够高效调用本地库。它将 Lisp 的生产力与 C++ 的性能结合，可能吸引对性能敏感或需要混合语言的项目。当前仍处于试验性阶段。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jank-lang.org/img/logo-text-dark.png' data-full='https://jank-lang.org/img/logo-text-dark.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50233304' data-tags='AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dailycal.org/news/campus/academics/ai-use-leads-uc-berkeley-math-professor-to-see-no-point-in-grading-homework/article_bdf1eae2-c08b-491f-b9f1-81a7cea177de.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI use leads UC Berkeley math professor to see &#x27;no point&#x27; in grading homework</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 使用让伯克利数学教授觉得作业评分没有意义</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50233304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 266</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A UC Berkeley math professor notes that widespread AI use among students undermines traditional grading, prompting a rethink of assessment methods and how to evaluate understanding in the age of AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州大学伯克利分校的一位数学教授指出，学生普遍使用人工智能使传统作业评分失去意义，促使人们重新思考评估方式以及在 AI 盛行时代如何衡量理解水平。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal essay examining how unemployment intersects with stress management and medical coping strategies, using cortisone as a lens. It reflects on resilience in the face of career upheaval and questions quick fixes in high-pressure tech cultures. The piece invites readers to consider healthier, long-term approaches to stress.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇个人随笔探讨失业如何与压力管理及医疗应对策略交织，以可的松为切入口进行思考。文章反思在高压科技环境中面对职业动荡时的韧性，以及对迅速解决问题心态的质疑，呼吁读者采用更健康、长期的压力管理方式。</p>
