<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neilmadden.blog/2026/04/24/java-sealed-classes-and-exhaustive-pattern-matching/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Java sealed classes and exhaustive pattern matching</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50141579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 138</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explains how sealed classes enable exhaustive pattern matching checks at compile time, reducing runtime errors. It also covers best practices for using sealed hierarchies with switch expressions and discusses trade-offs with openness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章阐述了密封类如何在编译时实现穷尽模式匹配，降低运行时错误，并提供使用密封层次结构与 switch 表达式的最佳实践，同时讨论了开放性带来的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alexsci.com/blog/uuidv7-is-weird/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UUIDv7: Timestamps aren’t time-ordered and other oddities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>UUIDv7：时间戳并非严格按顺序及其他怪异之处</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50211746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50211746' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UUIDv7 introduces time-based IDs, but timestamps can be non-monotonic and exhibit other oddities that affect ordering guarantees and system design choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>UUIDv7 引入基于时间的 ID，但时间戳可能并非严格按顺序，存在其他异常现象，影响排序保证和系统设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alexsci.com/blog/static/head-shot.png' data-full='https://alexsci.com/blog/static/head-shot.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.windowslatest.com/2026/10/19/why-windows-11-still-ships-with-windows-95s-memory-manager-hidden-inside-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Windows 11 still ships with Windows 95&#x27;s memory manager hidden inside it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为什么 Windows 11 仍然隐藏着 Windows 95 的内存管理器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50159726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Windows 11 still includes a memory manager borrowed from Windows 95 due to compatibility and maintenance concerns. The legacy component handles memory allocation for legacy subsystems, illustrating how modern OSes juggle performance with backward compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 11 仍然包含来自 Windows 95 的内存管理器，出于兼容性和维护成本考虑。该遗留组件为旧子系统提供内存管理，揭示了现代操作系统在性能与向后兼容之间的权衡。文章讨论了随着代码基的老化，设计决策的持续权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/How-Windows-95-code-survives-inside-Windows-11.jpg' data-full='https://www.windowslatest.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/How-Windows-95-code-survives-inside-Windows-11.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsd.network/@brynet/117407035545900963' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenBSD 8.0 release will have inteldrm/radeondrm/amdgpu drivers from Linux 6.18</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenBSD 8.0 将集成 Linux 6.18 的 inteldrm/radeondrm/amdgpu 驱动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50158138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 174</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50158138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 8.0 will import Linux 6.18 GPU drivers (inteldrm, radeondrm, amdgpu), expanding support for Intel/AMD GPUs. This move improves hardware compatibility and potentially performance on OpenBSD, but raises questions about Linux code dependencies, licensing, and security boundaries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenBSD 8.0 将把 Linux 6.18 的 GPU 驱动（inteldrm、radeondrm、amdgpu）引入到自身代码库中，扩大对英特尔/AMD 显卡的支持。这一举措提升了硬件兼容性与潜在性能，但也带来对 Linux 代码依赖、许可证与安全边界的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.bsd.network/accounts/avatars/000/001/413/original/d113a02fb84e4f5a.jpg' data-full='https://media.bsd.network/accounts/avatars/000/001/413/original/d113a02fb84e4f5a.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五个 Qwen3.8-27B 模型在 GVS5H harness 下达到与 Fable 5 相近的性能，展示了开源大模型在搭配高效硬件时的竞争力。此基准凸显了针对研究者和爱好者的优化部署堆栈的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Goose aims to offer a small, approachable language with strong safety guarantees and a pragmatic design. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Goose 编程语言旨在提供一种小巧、易学且具备强安全性保证的编程语言，设计更务实、易于使用。它在语言可用性和工具链方面提出了新的思路，可能吸引初学者与爱好者，同时也引发与现有生态系统互操作性的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teal-language.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teal – A statically-typed dialect of Lua</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Teal：Lua 的静态类型方言</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 170</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Teal adds a statically-typed layer to Lua, enabling compile-time checks and better editor tooling while remaining interoperable with existing Lua code. The project promotes optional typing and gradual adoption, helping teams catch errors earlier without abandoning Lua&#x27;s flexibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Teal 为 Lua 增添静态类型系统，能够进行编译期检查并提升编辑器支持，同时与现有 Lua 代码保持互操作。该语言强调可选类型与渐进式采用，帮助团队在不牺牲灵活性的前提下更早发现错误。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://teal-language.org/img/teal.svg' data-full='https://teal-language.org/img/teal.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PHP session.upload_progress heap UAF vulnerability (CVE-2026-109618/109619) could allow crashes or remote code execution. Patch PHP to affected versions and apply recommended mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PHP session.upload_progress 的堆上使用后释放漏洞（CVE-2026-109618/109619）可能导致崩溃或远程代码执行。请升级到受影响版本并应用推荐的缓解措施。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.githubstatus.com/incidents/8tzbpcnl75jp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Degraded availability for GitHub Actions, GitHub Pages and Webhooks</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50153934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50153934' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Actions, Pages, and Webhooks experienced degraded availability, disrupting CI/CD pipelines and site deployments. The incident highlights reliance on cloud services and the ripple effects when critical development tooling falters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Actions、Pages 与 Webhooks 服 务可用性下降，打断持续集成/部署流程和站点部署。事件凸显对云端服务的依赖，以及关键开发工具故障时的连锁影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aaron Ardiri has released PalmOS Lemmings, a faithful PalmOS port of the classic puzzle game Lemmings. It highlights retro game preservation and the ongoing hobbyist effort to run vintage software on aging mobile platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Aaron Ardiri 发布了 PalmOS Lemmings，这是对经典解谜游戏 Lemmings 的 PalmOS 版本移植，展示了对复古游戏的保护与在老旧移动平台上运行早期软件的热情。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们都生活在一部1970年鲜为人知的科幻电影中</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50214323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50214323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that contemporary life resembles a 1970 obscure sci-fi film, using it to critique tech culture, surveillance, and media narratives. The author uses comparison to prompt readers to rethink daily routines through sci-fi allegory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章把现代生活比喻成一部1970年鲜为人知的科幻片，用以批判科技文化、监控与媒体叙事。作者通过对比，促使读者重新审视日常生活中的技术依赖与隐私边界。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.jim-nielsen.com/2026/skip-retirement-for-utopia/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>No Need for Retirement When Utopia Is Around the Corner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>乌托邦近在眼前，退休并非必要</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50207050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50207050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that ongoing technological progress reduces the need to retire and encourages staying engaged through meaningful work or side projects. It champions lifelong learning and continuous opportunities as technology reshapes careers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章挑战退休作为人生终点的观念，认为持续的技术进步为人们提供了继续工作和参与有意义项目的机会。它倡导终身学习与持续的机会，以应对随着技术变革带来的职业前景变化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A laptop built around the 6502 CPU showcases retro computing in a portable form, blending classic hardware with modern peripherals. The project highlights how old architectures can still inspire hobbyist hardware, energy-efficient designs, and education about computer fundamentals. It also raises questions about performance, software compatibility, and DIY hardware culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这款笔记本以6502处理器为核心，将经典硬件带入便携设备。它展示了复古架构在便携化、低功耗设计和硬件爱好者教育中的潜力，同时也引发关于性能与软件兼容性的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(233) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codeberg.org/shitter/shitter' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shitter – A Nitter Fork</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Shitter：Nitter 的一个分叉实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 155</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50208343' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 152</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Shitter is a fork of Nitter, offering a privacy-conscious, JS-light front-end for Twitter-like content and a self-hosted viewing experience. It exemplifies open-source efforts to reduce tracking and API reliance while preserving access to public posts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Shitter 是 Nitter 的一个分叉，提供一个轻量、注重隐私的前端来浏览类似 Twitter 的内容，并支持自托管使用。该项目展示了开源社区在减少跟踪、降低对官方 API 依赖方面的努力，同时保持对公开内容的访问。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(238) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/health/2026/10/personalized-mrna-cancer-vaccine-cut-melanoma-death-recurrence-by-42/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Moderna and Merck say their mRNA cancer vaccine cut death, recurrence by 42%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>莫德纳和默克的 mRNA 癌症疫苗死亡与复发率下降 42%</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personalized mRNA cancer vaccine from Moderna and Merck reduced death and cancer recurrence by 42% in a clinical context, marking a meaningful advance in cancer immunotherapy and the application of mRNA tech beyond infectious disease.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>莫德纳与默克的个性化 mRNA 癌症疫苗在临床研究中将死亡率和复发率降低约 42%，这是癌症免疫治疗领域的一个重要进展，也展示了 mRNA 技术在抗癌领域的潜力。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canary Tokens provides decoy credentials and files that trigger alerts when accessed, enabling organizations to detect breaches early. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canary Tokens 提供可用于布置的诱饵凭据和伪文件，访问时会触发警报，帮助组织在入侵初期发现异常。该项目支持在不同环境中快速部署多种诱饵，并配合告警流程加强应急响应。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece investigates ethoxyquin, a fire retardant still used in some seafood supply chains, raising concerns about chemical exposure and safety standards. It highlights regulatory gaps and calls for greater transparency in seafood sourcing.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece explores procedural dungeon generation inspired by NetHack&#x27;s old-school roguelikes, focusing on designing mines filled with traps, monsters, and treasure. It discusses algorithms to generate varied, replayable levels and procedural lore.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/in-hilbert-space-all-things-are-quantumly-possible-20260826/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In Hilbert space, all things are quantumly possible</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在希尔伯特空间中，万事皆可量子化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hilbert space underpins the mathematics of quantum possibility, linking abstract functional analysis to what quantum computers might achieve. The piece explains how linear algebra concepts like superposition and interference drive quantum algorithms and why some problems become tractable on quantum hardware. It helps technologists gauge where quantum advantage could matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>希尔伯特空间构成了量子可能性的数学基础，将抽象的函数分析与量子计算可能达到的目标联系起来。文章阐述了线性代数中的叠加、干涉等概念如何推动量子算法，以及为何某些问题在量子硬件上变得可解。它帮助技术人员判断在哪些场景量子优势才会显现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/HilbertSpace-cr-Tommy-Parket-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/HilbertSpace-cr-Tommy-Parket-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50217683' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(247) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sciencedaily.com/releases/2026/10/261018084421.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alzheimer&#x27;s breakthrough: 2 hrs of sleep restored without clearing brain plaques</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿尔茨海默病突破：未清除脑斑时睡眠再现约两小时</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50217683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 139</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50217683' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study reports a breakthrough where about two hours of sleep are restored in an Alzheimer’s context without clearing brain plaques. This suggests that normalizing sleep may be possible independently from plaque clearance, offering a potential therapeutic angle for sleep management in Alzheimer&#x27;s.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示在未清除脑斑的情况下，睡眠时长约回升两小时，显示睡眠改善可能独立于斑块清除。此发现为睡眠治疗在阿尔茨海默病管理中的潜在方向提供了新线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/sleep-man-brain-spine-nervous-system.webp' data-full='https://www.sciencedaily.com/images/1920/sleep-man-brain-spine-nervous-system.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50222927' data-tags='Culture,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://de.numista.com/9866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The St. Helena 5 pence coin shows Jonathan, a roughly 190 year old tortoise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>圣赫勒拿岛5便士硬币印有乔纳森，一只约190年的象龟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50222927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 136</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50222927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The St. Helena 5 pence coin features Jonathan, the island&#x27;s famous tortoise believed to be around 190 years old. The coin highlights how long-lived individual animals can become cultural icons and bridge biology with numismatics, sparking interest among collectors and educators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>圣赫勒拿岛5便士硬币印有乔纳森——这只被认为约190岁的象龟，成为岛屿长寿动物的象征。该硬币展示了生物学与钱币收藏之间的交叉，激发收藏者与教育者的兴趣。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五角大楼批准国土安全部在边境测试热射线武器。此举引发关于安全性、法律与人道主义影响的讨论，也会影响公众对定向能源武器在民用场景中的监管与应用前景的看法。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A neural chip demonstrates decoding interactions between different brain regions, enabling more precise mapping of distributed processing. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究中的神经芯片实现了对大脑不同区域之间交互的解码，有望更精准地绘制分布式处理网络的协同关系。此项进展可能推动脑机接口的发展，并深化对大脑协同工作的认知。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://david.lequin.dev/blog/bi-software-is-dead/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can we replace a $150k Looker with a bucket of HTML files?</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50212320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50212320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Can a collection of HTML files replace a $150k Looker? The post argues for a cheaper, simpler BI approach using static HTML dashboards, but notes trade-offs in interactivity, freshness, and governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章提出以静态 HTML 把 Looker 替代的思路，质疑昂贵 BI 工具的必要性。尽管静态仪表盘价格低、维护简单，但在交互性、实时性和数据治理方面也存在取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(253) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/10/startup-taps-underwater-volcano-as-energy-source-in-proof-point-test/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Startup taps underwater volcano as energy source in “proof point” test</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>初创公司以海底火山作为能源进行实证测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50157456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50157456' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>One startup is harvesting energy from an underwater volcanic vent to run a proof-of-concept test. If successful, it could signal a path for offshore geothermal energy and extreme-environment power systems, though challenges include corrosion, reliability, and power transmission.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家初创公司正在海底火山喷口提取地热能以进行实证测试。若成功，可能为海上地热和极端环境能源系统铺路，但腐蚀、可靠性和能量传输等挑战仍需克服。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50096110' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.iwm.org.uk/history/iwm/cwr/telephone' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SIGSALY: Churchill&#x27;s top secret hotline to the president</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SIGSALY：丘吉尔与总统之间的绝密热线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50096110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50096110' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SIGSALY was a massive wartime cryptographic voice system that provided secure communications between Allied leaders. It represents an early, fully digital approach to encrypted telephony and marked a milestone in cryptography and secure data transmission.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SIGSALY 是二战时期的巨型数字化加密语音系统，为盟军领导人提供安全通信，标志着对话音 cryptography 的重要里程碑，也是早期数字化密钥传输的里程碑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.iwm.org.uk/sites/default/files/2022-11/churchill_and_guy_on_phone.jpg' data-full='https://www.iwm.org.uk/sites/default/files/2022-11/churchill_and_guy_on_phone.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coached.com/essays/most-advice-is-right-for-the-person-giving-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most advice is right for the person giving it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大多数建议对给出建议的人才是正确的</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 130</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50180444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 126</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece on why most advice resonates with the giver&#x27;s experiences, not the recipient&#x27;s, and the importance of asking tailored questions. Encourages critical thinking and testing suggestions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇反思文章指出，大多数建议往往更符合提出者的经历而非接受者的情境，强调提出量身定制的问题与评估。鼓励读者进行批判性思考并测试他人的建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.blast-info.fr/articles/2026/information-exclusive-blast-le-premier-ministre-a-prepare-la-coupure-des-reseaux-sociaux-pour-le-24-octobre-9D8cgrU5S5iiW14Sb9nMzQ' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France asked ISPs to prepare temporary DNS blocks for major social platforms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国要求互联网服务提供商为主要社交平台准备临时DNS封锁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50229861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50229861' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France asked ISPs to prepare temporary DNS blocks on major social platforms as a contingency measure for upcoming events. The move signals a willingness to deploy blunt censorship tools to curb platform influence, raising questions about free speech, digital sovereignty, and user impact. Observers will want to see how quickly such blocks could be activated and what safeguards exist.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国政府要求宽带运营商为主要社交平台预设临时DNS封锁，以应对即将到来的事件。此举显示当局愿意使用极端工具来控制舆论影响，同时引发关于言论自由、数字主权和对普通用户影响的讨论。将关注何时启用以及有哪些保障措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.blast-info.fr/stories/2026/open_graph-information-exclusive-blast-le-premier-ministre-a-prepare-la-coupure-des-reseaux-sociaux-pour-le-24-octobre-9D8cgrU5S5iiW14Sb9nMzQ.jpg' data-full='https://static.blast-info.fr/stories/2026/open_graph-information-exclusive-blast-le-premier-ministre-a-prepare-la-coupure-des-reseaux-sociaux-pour-le-24-octobre-9D8cgrU5S5iiW14Sb9nMzQ.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50185045' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hrih.si/articles/the-compiler-was-supposed-to-end-programming/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The compiler was supposed to end programming too</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编译器也该终结编程吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50185045' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50185045' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An op-ed questions whether the future of programming might be shaped by compilers that automate or suppress routine coding, exploring the tension between automation and human creativity. It envisions a shift toward higher-level tools and AI-assisted code generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇社论探讨编译器未来是否会通过自动化来减少人类编程工作，分析自动化与创意之间的张力。文章设想未来可能转向更高层次的工具和 AI 辅助的代码生成。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50138490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JAXA is preparing a Phobos sample-return mission to fetch material from Mars&#x27; moon and return it to Earth, a high-profile step toward answering questions about Mars and the early solar system. The mission would push engineering boundaries for in-space sample handling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JAXA 正筹划对火星卫星 Phobos 的样本返回任务，带回火星卫星材料以回答关于火星与早期太阳系的问题。这一任务将推进太空样本处理的工程边界。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dri.es/state-of-drupal-presentation-september-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Drupal is now light-years ahead of its reputation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Drupal 已经远超人们的印象</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50158408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50158408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Drupal has undergone modernization that boosts usability, security, and performance, challenging its reputation as outdated. The presentation highlights decoupled CMS options and a thriving ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Drupal 已经经过现代化改造，在易用性、安全性与性能方面有显著提升，打破了它被低估的形象。演示强调了无头 CMS 方案与蓬勃发展的生态系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dri.es/files/cache/drupalcon-rotterdam-2026/driesnote-1280w.png' data-full='https://dri.es/files/cache/drupalcon-rotterdam-2026/driesnote-1280w.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/php-vulnerabilities-waf-protection/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare deploys protections against php2shell vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare 部署对 php2shell 漏洞的防护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50181326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare has deployed protections against the php2shell vulnerability, adding WAF rules to block exploit chains that could lead to remote code execution. The update helps shield sites without user action.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 已部署 WAF 规则来阻挡 php2shell 漏洞的利用链，降低远程代码执行风险。更新无需用户额外操作即可提升站点安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M5FBPSP6GA0RRJ5FDQ68S476.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M5FBPSP6GA0RRJ5FDQ68S476.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50200164' data-tags='Web,Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.chrome.com/blog/html-in-canvas-origin-trial' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The HTML-in-Canvas API origin trial</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Canvas 内嵌 HTML API 的 Origin Trial</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50200164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Origin Trial for HTML-in-Canvas enables rendering HTML elements directly inside a canvas, enabling mixed-content UIs and richer visual effects. It raises considerations around performance, accessibility, and security as web apps blend DOM and canvas content. If adopted, developers could push more creative UI patterns while needing careful feature support deprecation planning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HTML-in-Canvas API 的 Origin Trial 让 HTML 元素直接嵌入画布中，使 DOM 与画布内容混合呈现成为可能。该特性在提升视觉表现力的同时，也带来性能、可访问性与安全性的权衡。若最终落地，开发者将能探索更丰富的 UI 模式，同时需留意后续的兼容与弃用规划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer.chrome.com/static/blog/html-in-canvas-origin-trial/image/thumbnail.png' data-full='https://developer.chrome.com/static/blog/html-in-canvas-origin-trial/image/thumbnail.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ratfactor.com/cards/limitations' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Importance of Limitations for Creativity and Learning (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>限制对创造力与学习的重要性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50225663' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Constraints act as catalysts for creativity and learning by forcing focus and iteration within practical bounds. The piece argues that setting limits helps learners and makers discover efficient paths, test edge cases, and accelerate skill development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>约束条件促使创造力与学习，因为它们迫使在现实边界内进行聚焦与迭代。文章指出设定界限能帮助学习者和开发者发现高效路径、测试边界情形，并推动技能快速成长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ratfactor.com/images/rat-logo.svg' data-full='https://ratfactor.com/images/rat-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(276) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.oist.jp/news-center/news/2026/9/18/new-form-plastic-pollution-discovered-shores-vulnerable-coral-reefs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New plastic pollution discovered: &#x27;Plasticorals&#x27; coral rubble fused with plastic</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型塑料污染：Plasticorals 珊瑚礁碎块与塑料融合</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50183512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50183512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 51</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report a new form of plastic pollution where coral rubble fuses with plastic, creating &#x27;Plasticorals&#x27;. The finding hints at a troubling feedback loop harming reef recovery and complicating cleanup, underscoring urgent plastic management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者发现一种新的塑料污染形态：珊瑚礁碎块与塑料融合，形成所谓的 Plasticorals。这一现象可能阻碍珊瑚礁恢复，并使清理工作更为复杂，凸显紧急的塑料治理需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.oist.jp/sites/default/files/styles/open_graph/public/2026-09/Sosu1.png?h=65743cbf&amp;itok=rKZ-K4sp' data-full='https://www.oist.jp/sites/default/files/styles/open_graph/public/2026-09/Sosu1.png?h=65743cbf&amp;itok=rKZ-K4sp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(277) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://calabro.io/ohbf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>One Hash Bloom Filters</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>单哈希布隆过滤器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50157865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50157865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of a Bloom filter variant that uses a single hash function, reducing memory and computation while preserving useful false-positive rates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章提出一种只需单一哈希函数即可实现的布隆过滤器变体，进一步降低内存和计算开销，同时保持可控的误报率。适用于资源受限的系统中高效的集合成员资格检测，并讨论了权衡与应用场景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://calabro.io/static/posts/bsky-bloom-filter-positive-rate.png' data-full='https://calabro.io/static/posts/bsky-bloom-filter-positive-rate.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2026/10/8087-addition-subtraction-microcode.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Intel 8087 chip: floating-point addition and subtraction</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英特尔 8087 浮点加减运算的微码揭秘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50142449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50142449' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article dives into the 8087 microcode path for floating-point addition and subtraction, revealing early FP unit design tricks that made real-time math feasible on x86. It connects these traces to the evolution of modern FP performance and architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章深入分析 8087 浮点加法/减法的微码路径，揭示早期浮点单元设计中的巧妙之处，使得 x86 上的实时浮点运算成为可能，并将这些细节与现代浮点性能及架构的演进联系起来。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemma-4-26B-A4B：在同等准确度下减少 39-69% 的推理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50209907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50209907' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A claim that Gemma-4-26B-A4B achieves significantly lower thinking requirements at the same accuracy, signaling efficiency gains for AI deployments. The note discusses implications for latency, cost, and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>称 Gemma-4-26B-A4B 在保持同等准确度的前提下，推理成本降低 39-69%，展现了部署效率的潜力，并影响时延、成本与安全性考量。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PHP Web 运行时漏洞（CVE-2026-109618 与 CVE-2026-109619）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50187708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50187708' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AWS security bulletin warns of PHP web runtimes with two CVEs; patches released; impact: remote code execution or information disclosure possible; organizations should update to patched runtimes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AWS 安全公告指出某些 PHP Web 运行时存在 CVE-2026-109618 与 CVE-2026-109619。已发布修补，可能导致远程代码执行或信息泄露。请尽快更新到修补版本。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://engelsbergideas.com/notebook/gdynia-and-the-dream-of-a-maritime-poland/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gdynia and the Dream of a Maritime Poland</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>格丁尼亚与波兰的海洋梦想</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50124811' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gdynia’s rise as a major Baltic port reflects Poland’s postwar modernization and its maritime ambitions. The piece traces urban planning, port governance, and logistics evolution that positioned Gdynia within Europe’s sea-trade network.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>格丁尼亚成为波兰波罗的海地区重要港口的崛起，体现战后现代化进程与海洋抱负。文章梳理了城市规划、港口治理与物流体系的演变，以及其在欧洲海上贸易网络中的地位。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(282) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thunk.blog/posts/types-of-types-haskell-kind-system/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Types of types: Haskell&#x27;s Kind system</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Haskell 的 Kind 系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50090927' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 76</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delves into the Haskell kind system, explaining how kinds classify type constructors and support higher-kinded types. It clarifies the role of kinds in enabling advanced type-level programming and ensuring kind-correctness in generic code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文介绍 Haskell 的 Kind（类型的类型）系统，解释如何用来分类类型构造器并支撑高阶类型。通过示例，阐明 Kind 在实现高级类型级编程和保证通用代码的类型正确性中的作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(285) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-025-58290-8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Restoration of Retinal Regenerative Potential (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>视网膜再生潜力的修复（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140111' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 2025 study reports restoration of retinal regenerative potential, showing molecular cues that reactivate dormant regeneration pathways in retinal cells. If reproducible, this could inform therapies for degenerative diseases like AMD by promoting endogenous repair rather than implants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项 2025 年的研究报道视网膜再生潜力的恢复，揭示能重新激活视网膜细胞中潜伏再生通路的分子线索。若能在更广泛的模型中复现，可能为退行性视网膜疾病如黄斑变性提供以自体修复为核心的治疗路线。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41467-025-58290-8/MediaObjects/41467_2025_58290_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41467-025-58290-8/MediaObjects/41467_2025_58290_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50131315' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(291) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-10-rabbits-medieval-royal-luxury-england.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rabbits were a medieval royal luxury in England until the Black Death</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国中世纪的皇家奢侈：兔子直至黑死病前都是珍品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 104</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rabbits were a status symbol for English royalty in the Middle Ages, farmed in elaborate warrens and treated as scarce luxury meat. The Black Death upended aristocratic diets and economies, reshaping what counted as luxury and who could afford it. The piece underscores how pandemics can abruptly shift supply chains and social hierarchies around food.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中世纪时，兔子被英国王室视作高端奢侈品，养在专门的兔养场，供给稀缺且价格昂贵。随着黑死病的爆发，贵族饮食和经济结构遭到重创，奢侈品的地位和获取方式也随之改变。此次发现强调疫情如何迅速打乱食物供应链与社会等级。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/a-rabbit-riding-on-a-s.jpg' data-full='https://scx2.b-cdn.net/gfx/news/hires/2026/a-rabbit-riding-on-a-s.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(292) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.boristhebrave.com/2026/10/18/chain-of-thought-for-humans/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chain of Thought for Humans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chain of Thought for Humans</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 103</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Drawing parallels between AI chain-of-thought prompts and human reasoning, the piece argues that externally detailing step-by-step thought processes can improve human problem solving. It offers practical practices for structuring thinking and recording reasoning, similar to how prompts guide AI thinking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文将 AI 的连锁推理与人类推理联系起来，主张通过外显分步推理来提升人类解决问题的能力，并给出将推理过程结构化并记录的实用做法。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50160877' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(293) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/golang/go/issues/82345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Go will reject certificate chains containing eight distrusted SPKIs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Go 将拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Go is tightening TLS validation by rejecting certificate chains that include eight distrusted SPKI entries. The change reduces attack surface from compromised keys and misissued certificates, nudging developers to audit complex chains or intermediates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Go 将通过在 TLS 验证中拒绝包含八个不可信 SPKI 的证书链，来收紧安全性。此举降低了被入侵密钥和被篡改证书带来的风险，促使开发者审查包含多中间证书的证书链。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e0de7325f0958bc60dbec5dbaa2b9b4590deee095e534c097bf4c3bc57cba2bc/golang/go/issues/82345' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/e0de7325f0958bc60dbec5dbaa2b9b4590deee095e534c097bf4c3bc57cba2bc/golang/go/issues/82345' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50140383' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(294) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://verdagon.dev/blog/reverse-borrow-checking' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new way to think about borrow checking (in reverse)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从反向视角看借用检查的新思路</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50140383' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new take on borrow checking flips the traditional perspective, examining lifetimes by tracing how data flows backward from usage. The approach aims to simplify reasoning about aliasing, mutability, and borrows, potentially easing compiler checks without sacrificing safety. It could influence future language design and verification tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章从相反的角度重新思考借用检查，逆向追踪数据的使用路径来分析生命周期。该方法旨在简化对别名、可变性和借用的推理，或在不牺牲安全性的前提下让编译器校验更易于实现，可能影响未来语言设计与验证工具。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50199230' data-tags='Science,AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(295) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27251' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI and the Enclosure of the Scientific Commons</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 与科学公共领域的封闭化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50199230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50199230' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This paper argues that AI practice risks enclosing the scientific commons by privatizing data, models, and tools, threatening open science and reproducibility. It surveys policy, legal, and technical avenues to preserve openness as AI capabilities grow.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为 AI 实践通过私有化数据、模型与工具正在逐步封闭科学公共领域，威胁开放科学与可重复性。作者从政策、法律与技术路径探讨在 AI 能力扩张阶段如何维持开放与公平获取。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dns.museum' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The DNS Museum</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The DNS Museum curates artifacts and explanations tracing the history and evolution of the Domain Name System, highlighting milestones from early research to modern DNS security practices. For developers and businesses, it underscores how DNS design decisions ripple through internet performance and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DNS 博物馆收集并展示域名系统的历史文献与展品，梳理从早期研究到现今 DNS 安全实践的关键节点。通过互动和叙述，帮助开发者和企业理解 DNS 的演进及其对互联网的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>具代理性的监控</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thought-provoking piece arguing that monitoring systems should be agentic — capable of taking initiative, not just reporting signals. It discusses design, control, and safety implications for AI governance and autonomous systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇博文提出监控系统应具备代理性，能够主动采取行动，而不仅是被动汇报。文章讨论了在 AI 治理和自治系统中的设计、控制与安全含义。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(300) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/triplet-of-ghostly-galaxies-hints-at-what-dark-matter-is-and-isnt-20261014/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Triplet of &#x27;ghostly&#x27; galaxies hints at what dark matter is and isn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三座“幽灵”星系揭示暗物质的可能属性与局限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50131997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astronomers study a trio of dim, ghostly galaxies that challenge and refine dark matter models. The unusual dynamics in these faint systems help constrain which properties dark matter could or could not have.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>天文学家研究三座暗淡的“幽灵”星系，这组观测对暗物质模型提出挑战并提供更精确的约束。它们的异常动力学有助于界定暗物质可能具备或不具备的性质。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Overgrown is a browser extension that animates plant life on your idle tabs, turning cluttered bookmarks into a tiny garden.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50192975' data-tags='Programming,Hardware,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.infoq.com/news/2026/10/tornadovm-7-gpu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TornadoVM 7 Enables GPU-Native Workloads from Java Applications</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TornadoVM 使 Java 应用能够直接在 GPU 上运行本地工作负载</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50192975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50192975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TornadoVM 7 enables GPU-native workloads to run directly from Java applications, reducing the need for JNI or native wrappers. This broadens Java-based GPU acceleration for data science and HPC tasks, and could simplify performance-critical code paths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TornadoVM 7 使 Java 应用能够直接在 GPU 上运行本地工作负载，减少对 JNI 或本地包装的依赖。这拓展了 Java 在数据科学和高性能计算中的 GPU 加速能力，并可能简化对性能关键代码路径的优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.infoq.com/statics_s2_20261020104415/styles/static/images/logo/logo-big.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.infoq.com/statics_s2_20261020104415/styles/static/images/logo/logo-big.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50235017' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.alfie.wtf/programming/why-i-am-a-programmer.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why I Am a Programmer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我为何成为程序员</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective essay explaining the author&#x27;s reasons for choosing programming, focusing on curiosity, problem solving, and the craft of building impactful software. It invites readers to see programming as a meaningful, creative pursuit.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇反思性文章讲述作者为何选择成为程序员，强调好奇心、解决问题的能力以及打造有影响力软件的工匠精神。呼吁读者把编程视为一种有意义、富有创造性的追求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.csis.org/analysis/how-quickly-can-dod-rebuild-and-recast-munitions-industrial-base' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Quickly Can the DoD Rebuild and Recast the Munitions Industrial Base?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>国防部多快能重建并重塑军品工业基础？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50146624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 89</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50146624' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 105</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CSIS analyzes the timeline and challenges for rebuilding the DoD&#x27;s munitions industrial base, emphasizing supply-chain resilience, domestic manufacturing, workforce, and policy alignment. It underscores this as a long, multi-year effort requiring sustained investment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>CSIS 的分析围绕美国国防部重建与重塑军品工业基地的时间线及挑战，强调供应链韧性、本土制造能力、劳动力和产业政策等关键要素。结论认为这是一项，需要多年投入与政策协调的长期工作。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A working paper questions why meetings proliferate, showing limited marginal gains and high cognitive cost. It suggests practical tweaks to reduce meeting fatigue.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇工作论文质疑为何会议如此普遍，显示边际收益有限且认知成本高，提出减少会议疲劳的可行改进。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(308) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quentinkaiser.be/security/2026/10/16/one-parser-two-bugs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>One parser, two bugs, many routers: Exploiting Broadcom WiFi across eCos, Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个解析器，两个漏洞，众多路由器：Broadcom WiFi 漏洞覆盖 eCos 与 Linux</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A parser flaw in Broadcom WiFi stacks leads to two bugs that affect eCos and Linux-based routers. The write-up outlines practical exploitation paths and what firmware mitigations and vendor patches should address.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Broadcom WiFi 栈中的一个解析器缺陷导致两处漏洞，影响基于 eCos 与 Linux 的路由器。文章给出可行的利用路径，并就固件缓解措施和厂商修补提出建议。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://forums.plex.tv/t/2026-20-0-beta-ios-and-tvos-4k-dovi-hdr10-won-t-play-infinite-spinning-circle/943966?page=5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Plex laid off the whole player team</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Plex 裁掉整個播放器团队</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50186318' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Plex laid off the entire player team, signaling a strategic pivot away from consumer-facing player apps. The move could affect platform support, mobile and smart-TV experiences, and raises questions about the company’s roadmap and developer ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Plex 已解散整个播放器团队，显示其在消费者端播放器应用上的战略转向。此举可能影响应用、移动端与智能电视体验，并引发对其路线图与开发者生态的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(310) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/claude-scorpion-toxin-binder-design' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude designed protein binders against scorpion neurotoxins</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50170021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 87</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50170021' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s Claude-driven design identifies protein binders that target scorpion neurotoxins, illustrating AI-assisted therapeutic design potential. The work points toward faster, AI-enabled antidote development while underscoring the need for rigorous validation and safety safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的 Claude 驱动的蛋白设计发现了可结合蝎子神经毒素的结合物，展示了 AI 在治疗设计方面的潜力。该工作提示能更快速地开发解毒剂，但也强调需要严格的验证与安全防护。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Footage from a Flock search—an automated license-plate or facial recognition tool—was ruled unconstitutional by a judge, highlighting ongoing civil-liberties concerns around enforcement tech and warrants. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一段关于 Flock 搜索的录像因被法官裁定违宪而曝光，凸显了对公共监控技术在执法中的广泛使用以及隐私与宪法权利的担忧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(314) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zion-sati/NetWasm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C# now beats Rust on WASM size</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>C# 在 WASM 大小方面现在比 Rust 更小</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50175510' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A WASM size comparison suggests C# compiled to WebAssembly can produce smaller binaries than Rust for certain workloads, challenging the assumption that Rust always yields the smallest output. This influences language choice for wasm-based apps, though ecosystem and performance considerations remain important.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对比显示在某些工作负载下，C# 编译为 WebAssembly 的二进制体积可能比 Rust 更小，挑战了“Rust 总是最小”的常识。对 wasm 应用的语言选择产生影响，但生态和性能等因素仍然重要。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(316) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/omnara-ai/omnara' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Omnara – Open-source alternative to Claude Managed Agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Omnara Open source 替代 Claude 管理代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50183892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50183892' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Omnara is an open source alternative to Claude&#x27;s Managed Agents, offering configurable AI agents that you can run and customize yourself. The project emphasizes transparency and privacy and aims to match managed agent capabilities with self hosted deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Omnara 是一个开源替代方案，定位为可本地控管的 Claude Managed Agents 的对等实现，提供可配置的 AI 代理和可插拔集成。该项目强调透明度、可定制性和隐私，面向希望自托管 AI 自动化的团队。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anubhavmalhotra/r2d2-tv' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Making a smart TV dumb and then smart</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：把智能电视变得更“傻”再重新变为智能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 84</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50202865' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY project that strips embedded smart TV software to create a privacy-preserving, lean device, then reintroduces smart capabilities in a more controllable way. The effort highlights the tension between vendor-bloat and user autonomy, and points to hackable hardware routes for better privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 DIY 项目通过移除智能电视的内置软件来打造一个轻量、注重隐私的设备，随后再以更可控的方式重新引入智能功能。此举凸显厂商臃肿软件与用户自主权之间的矛盾，并指向可 Hack 的硬件方式以提升隐私保护。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(324) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cambridge.org/core/journals/antiquity/article/social-dynamics-of-toxic-metal-industry-in-medieval-europe-peak-district-lead-production-ad-6001500/34A2A16AC99B1B12E3EB99CEA1DDB829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Social dynamics of toxic metal industry in medieval Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>中世纪欧洲有毒金属产业的社会动态：峰区铅生产（公元600-1500）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50169450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article analyzes how toxic metal production shaped medieval European society, using the Peak District lead industry (AD 600–1500) as a case study. It discusses labor organization, health impacts, and local economies, offering insight into how hazardous industry influenced social dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该研究以峰区铅产业（公元 600–1500 年）为案例，分析有毒金属生产如何塑造中世纪欧洲的社会结构。论文探讨劳动力组织、健康风险与地方经济，揭示危险行业对社会动态的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xbox reportedly turned down Netflix’s $500 million acquisition offer, signaling a strategic focus on its own ecosystem and original content. The move highlights the tension between gaming platforms and streaming businesses and hints at broader consolidation dynamics in entertainment tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Xbox 拒绝了 Netflix 提出的 5 亿美元收购要约，显示出其在发展自家生态与原创内容方面的战略聚焦。这一举动凸显了游戏平台与流媒体业务之间的互相牵制，并透露了娱乐科技领域的并购动态。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nsidc.org/learn/ask-scientist/karakoram-anomaly-it-real' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What Is the Karakoram Anomaly?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>卡拉科姆异常是什么？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 76</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50160241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers describe the Karakoram Anomaly, where glaciers in the Karakoram range are not retreating as quickly as global trends, with some stability or growth. Ongoing work points to snowfall, glacier dynamics, and topography, bearing implications for water security in the region.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>卡拉科姆异常指的是卡拉科姆山脉的冰川退缩速度低于全球平均，部分区域甚至稳定或增长。研究持续探索降雪、冰川动力和地形等因素，对南亚地区的水资源安全具有重要影响。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(332) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.biograph.com/newsroom/biograph-completes-first-u.s.-study-with-caristo-diagnostics%E2%80%99-ai-powered-coronary-inflammation-detection-finding-risk-in-over-42-of-asymptomatic-patients' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI analysis of CT scans finds 42.9% of patients have coronary inflammation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 分析 CT 扫描发现 42.9% 患者存在冠状炎症</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50198940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 74</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50198940' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI-powered study analyzing CT scans reports a 42.9% prevalence of coronary inflammation in the studied cohort, suggesting AI-assisted radiology could flag cardiovascular risk earlier. Results warrant clinical validation and broader cohorts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项使用 AI 分析 CT 扫描的研究显示，在研究人群中有 42.9% 的患者存在冠状炎症，提示 AI 辅助放射学可能帮助及早识别心血管风险。该结果需在更大规模人群中进行临床验证。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Beyond the ampersand, this piece explores more robust patterns for managing asynchronous work in Linux. It covers job control, pipelines, and modern tools that provide richer concurrency primitives and safer process management than relying on backgrounding with &amp; alone.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨如何在 Linux 系统中超越单纯使用符号 &amp; 的后台任务管理，介绍更稳健的异步工作模式。内容涉及作业控制、管道设计、现代 shell 以及提供更丰富并发原语的工具，提升进程管理安全性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deathplanswitch.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Death Plan Switch, a self-hosted dead man&#x27;s switch for solo devs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Claude Code 的 Lights-on 软件工厂样板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50161408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 72</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50161408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Death Plan Switch offers a self-hosted dead-man&#x27;s switch for solo developers to securely hand off critical data, keys, or code to trusted contacts if they become unavailable. It provides a privacy-preserving, local-first workflow that avoids relying on closed, external services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Death Plan Switch 为独立开发者提供一个自托管的死线开关，方便在无法工作时把关键数据、密钥或代码安全移交给信任的联系人。它采用隐私保护、本地优先的工作流，尽量避免对外部服务的依赖。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50150935' data-tags='Science,Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(340) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/esa-hera-dimorphos-rendezvous' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ESA&#x27;s Hera Will View Asteroid Scars from DART&#x27;s Strike</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ESA 的 Hera 将观测 DART 碰撞后的小行星伤痕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50150935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ESA&#x27;s Hera mission will rendezvous with the Didymos system to image and measure the crater created by NASA&#x27;s DART impact. The data will validate impact models and help calibrate planetary-defense techniques for future mitigation strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲航天局的 Hera 任务将与双小行星系统会合，拍摄并测量 DART 碰撞形成的陨坑。数据有助于校验碰撞模型、校准行星防御策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/3d-rendering-of-a-space-probe-approaching-two-asteroids.jpg?id=68453287&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C172%2C0%2C413' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/3d-rendering-of-a-space-probe-approaching-two-asteroids.jpg?id=68453287&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C172%2C0%2C413' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cam.ac.uk/research/news/rise-of-collagen-eating-microbes-may-help-explain-gaps-in-the-fossil-record' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rise of collagen-eating microbes may help explain gaps in the fossil record</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>胶原蛋白分解微生物的崛起可能解释化石记录中的空档</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50168075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50168075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research suggests collagen-degrading microbes may bias the fossil record, offering a microbial lens on preservation and how some organisms vanish from the geological timeline.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究表明，胶原蛋白分解微生物可能导致化石记录的偏差，从微生物角度解释某些生物在地质时间线上的消失。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cam.ac.ukGettyImages-1359393158 dp.jpg' data-full='https://www.cam.ac.ukGettyImages-1359393158 dp.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
