<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://microship.com/lisp-comes-of-age/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LISP Comes of Age (1980)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LISP 成熟：1980 年代的演进</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50199684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50199684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on Lisp&#x27;s maturation in the 1980s, tracing its influence on AI, language design, and education. It reflects on enduring Lisp ideas like homoiconicity, macros, and REPL-driven development and why Lisp remains influential.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 Lisp 在 1980 年代的成熟阶段，追踪其对 AI、语言设计和教育的影响。反思 Lisp 的核心理念，如同构性、宏机制与 REPL 驱动开发，以及其持续影响力的原因。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Python 与 Julia 中的符号学习</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50171936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50171936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PySR blends symbolic regression with modern ML ecosystems to yield interpretable models in Python and Julia. By combining mathematical discovery with scalable tooling, it helps uncover human-readable expressions that fit data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PySR 将符号回归与现代机器学习生态系统相结合，在 Python 与 Julia 中生成可解释的模型。通过将数学发现与可扩展工具链结合，它有助于发现能拟合数据且易读的表达式。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对亚马逊为幼儿设计的“数字安抚物”提出批评，认为以屏幕安抚儿童的做法可能带来隐私与成长成本。文章权衡数据收集与注意力风险，并提出更为谨慎的早教方式。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings suggest ovaries stay metabolically active after menopause, challenging assumptions about postmenopausal physiology. The study opens questions about aging, hormone regulation, and potential therapeutic angles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新发现表明更年期后卵巢仍具代谢活性，挑战了对绝经后生理的传统认知。研究引发对衰老、激素调控及潜在治疗方向的新问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hbsslaw.com/cases/verisign-com-domain-name-registry-consumer-class-action' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Verisign .com Domain Name Registry Consumer Class Action</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50148441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50148441' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Consumers allege Verisign&#x27;s management of the .com registry harmed users, possibly around pricing, renewal practices, or security; the action could lead to refunds or changes in terms. If successful, the case could set precedents for how registry operators are regulated and held accountable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>消费者对 Verisign 的 .com 域名注册管理提起集体诉讼，指控在价格、续费策略或安全等方面损害用户利益。案件若获胜，可能带来赔偿和对注册商条款的调整，对域名持有者具有现实影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Subaru announces a Europe-only pivot to electric vehicles, exiting traditional internal-combustion models in the region. The move reflects regulatory pressures and the accelerating transition to EVs, with ripple effects for dealers, supply chains, and regional market strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Subaru 宣布在欧洲仅售电动汽车，停止在该地区销售传统燃油车型。此举折射出日益增压的监管要求以及电动化转型的加速，对经销商、供应链与区域市场策略都会带来连锁影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Indie webcomics face increasing censorship online, with moderation, platform policies, and ad revenue changes squeezing small creators. Creators respond by crowdfunding, self-hosting, and migrating to alternative platforms, underscoring the fragility of independent voices in a crowded web.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立网络漫画家正面临日益严苛的网络审查、平台政策调整和广告收入下降带来的生存压力。作者们转向自建站点、众筹和替代平台等方式维持创作与传播，凸显网络出版领域的多元声音正在受挤压。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(375) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danielmangum.com/posts/mcu-emudhra-intermediate-ca-compromise/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your MCU likely escaped the eMudhra intermediate CA compromise</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的 MCU 很可能躲过了 eMudhra 中间 CA 的妥协</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50183280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 59</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50183280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The eMudhra intermediate CA compromise is unlikely to affect most MCUs, thanks to certificate chains and firmware signing practices. Still, devices relying on that chain may need updates; vendors outline mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由于证书链和固件签名等做法，大多数微控制器很可能未受 eMudhra 中间 CA 事件的影响。但若设备依赖该链的信任，则可能需要更新，厂商给出相应的缓解措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://danielmangum.com/images/twitter-card.png' data-full='https://danielmangum.com/images/twitter-card.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(376) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Laminar (YC S24) – RL-trained model that finds errors in agent traces</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>推出 HN：Laminar（YC S24）— 强化学习训练的模型用于发现代理轨迹中的错误</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50154029' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Laminar, a RL-trained model from YC S24, identifies errors in agent traces, enabling faster debugging of RL systems. It helps reduce manual inspection and improve reliability, though it raises questions about training data quality and coverage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Laminar 是 YC S24 的强化学习训练模型，能够在代理轨迹中发现错误。该工具有望加速 RL 系统的调试、减少人工检查，但也带来关于训练数据质量与覆盖范围的考虑。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/how-do-fungi-keep-the-planet-healthy-20261015/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Do Fungi Keep the Planet Healthy?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>真菌如何让地球保持健康？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 56</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50134809' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fungi play crucial roles as decomposers, symbionts, and biotechnological agents. They support nutrient cycling, plant health, and ecosystem resilience against climate change, through networks and metabolic processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>真菌在分解、共生关系和生物技术应用中发挥关键作用，支撑养分循环、植物健康与生态系统对气候变化的韧性。文中介绍了菌丝网络在土壤健康与碳循环中的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust off Rails advocates for a Rust-based, opinionated web stack tailored to AI-era workloads, emphasizing safety and predictable performance over conventional Rails. It argues for fewer runtime surprises in large-scale apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust off Rails 推介以 Rust 为基础、具有明确理念的 Web 技术栈，面向 AI 时代的工作负载，强调安全性与可预见的高性能，旨在替代传统 Rails。主张在大规模应用中减少运行时的不确定性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A German court ruled that Google&#x27;s aerial imagery constitutes spying, highlighting privacy and data collection tensions in mapping services. The decision could influence regulation of street-level data collection in Germany.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delays in PRs and webhook deliveries indicate a service incident affecting code review and CI/CD workflows. The outage underscores the need for reliable event delivery and retry strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>拉取请求与 Webhook 的交付延迟表明出现了服务事件，会影响代码评审和 CI/CD 工作流。这暴露了事件传递的韧性与重试机制的需求。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(398) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/10/24/the-state-of-context-engineering.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The State of Context Engineering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>上下文工程现状</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The State of Context Engineering surveys how teams manage context in software and AI workflows, from memory and retrieval-augmented techniques to dynamic prompts and tool integration. It highlights practical patterns, common pitfalls, and what’s needed to scale context without losing performance or privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理了在软件和 AI 工作流程中如何管理上下文，包括记忆、检索增强、动态提示和工具整合等，并指出放大上下文时在性能与隐私方面需要关注的常见陷阱与有效做法。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(399) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://e360.yale.edu/features/2026-film-contest-second-place-cerrado' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Bicycle Journey Across Brazil to Find a Vanishing Savanna</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>穿越巴西寻找消失草原的自行车之旅</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50177554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A documentary-styled piece about a cyclist traversing Brazil to document the Cerrado, highlighting biodiversity loss, conservation challenges, and human stories. It underscores the fragility of this bioregion and the need for stewardship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过一段横跨巴西的自行车旅程记录 Cerrado 大草原的生物多样性丧失与保护挑战，聚焦自然与人的故事，强调这一生物区域的脆弱性与保护必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.ael8752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Demographic &quot;doomsday&quot; misses the mark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人口‘末日论’并未如预期般成立</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 50</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50165276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demographic &#x27;doomsday&#x27; predictions miss the mark as new analyses highlight more nuanced trends in fertility, aging, and migration. The piece argues that policy responses should focus on resilience and adaptation rather than alarm.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人口末日论被新的分析所否定，揭示生育、人口老龄化与迁移等趋势的更细腻层面。文章主张以韧性与适应性为导向的政策应对，而非惊慌。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude now integrates with Google Docs, Sheets, and Slides, letting users generate text, summarize content, and perform data tasks directly within the apps. This tight integration boosts productivity by enabling natural-language prompts and AI-assisted edits in familiar workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 已与 Google 文档、表格和幻灯片深度集成，允许在应用中直接用自然语言生成文本、摘要及处理数据。此集成提升了工作效率，使 AI 辅助的编辑与分析嵌入日常工作流程。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pillowfort.social/posts/8413273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pillowfort Is Closing</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50176301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50176301' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pillowfort is shutting down, prompting users to export data and consider alternatives. The move underscores the fragility of independent social platforms and the importance of data portability for communities. It also raises questions about how niche networks can sustain themselves long term.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pillowfort 将关闭，要求用户导出数据并考虑替代方案。这一举措凸显独立社交平台的脆弱性，以及数据可移植性对社区的重要性。与此同时，也引发了小众网络长期可持续发展的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(407) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quantum5.ca/2025/03/22/whirlwind-tour-of-systemd-nspawn-containers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A whirlwind tour of systemd-nspawn containers (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>systemd-nspawn 容器快速入门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50214983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50214983' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise tour of systemd-nspawn containers, covering setup, image formats, isolation features, and typical use cases from development to testing. It also contrasts its approach with Docker-like tooling in systemd-focused environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 systemd-nspawn 容器进行快速概览，涵盖设置、镜像格式、隔离特性以及从开发到测试的典型用例，并对比在 systemd 环境中的 Docker 式工具。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study benchmarks how current bot-detection systems perform against sophisticated AI agents in 2026, revealing evolving evasion techniques and mixed detector effectiveness. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究基准测试了当前的机器人检测系统在对抗2026年先进 AI 代理时的表现，揭示了不断进化的规避手段与检测器效果的差异性。研究结果强调在 moderation 与安全工具中需要更具适应性的多层防御。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that software provenance and behavior can be understood even without source code, exploring techniques like binary analysis, instrumentation, and behavior-based security. It emphasizes practical implications for defenders and researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出即使没有源代码，也能通过二进制分析、动态分析与行为驱动的安全方法来理解软件行为。强调对防守方与研究人员在受限或闭源软件场景下的实际应用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As plasma displays gave way to LCD/LED, their era is remembered for deep blacks and fast motion, but high production costs and burn-in contributed to decline. The piece traces the rise, peak, and fall of plasma screens and what lessons it leaves for display tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>等离子屏曾以深黑色和出色运动表现著称，但高成本和烧屏等问题推动其衰落。文章回顾等离子屏的兴起、巅峰及衰退，并讨论对显示技术的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50204125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50204125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports a shrinking median product lifespan as markets mature and platforms commoditize, forcing faster iteration cycles and more rapid sunset of features. Implications for product strategy, retention, and platform governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>中位产品寿命缩短，市场趋于成熟与平台同质化，迫使更快的迭代和更快的功能淘汰。这对产品策略、用户留存和平台治理有深远影响。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PHP has announced an upcoming critical security release to address multiple high-severity vulnerabilities, including possible remote code execution scenarios. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PHP 发布即将到来的关键安全更新，修补多项高危漏洞，可能涉及远程代码执行。运行 PHP 的网站应尽快应用补丁，以降低被利用的风险，因为未打补丁的站点仍然是常见的攻击向量。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gheist campaign abused stolen GitHub tokens to plant secret-dumping workflows, highlighting how token theft can undermine CI/CD pipelines. The write-up urges rotation, least privilege, and token hygiene to limit impact.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that focusing on small, incremental changes can dramatically accelerate code review cycles while preserving quality. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张通过聚焦小而增量的改动，可以显著加速代码审查周期并保持质量。提出最小化差异、预提交检查以及提交规范等做法，减轻审查者的认知负担。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres recovery_prefetch 可能引发中断</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50188614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50188614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A PostgreSQL feature called recovery_prefetch can, in some cases, cause outages when misconfigured or buggy. The post recommends caution, disabling in production, and applying patches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PostgreSQL 的 recovery_prefetch 功能在配置不当或实现存在缺陷时可能导致服务中断。建议在生产环境中谨慎使用、必要时禁用并及时应用修补程序。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Poll findings show broad concern about AI&#x27;s societal impact and resistance to expanding data-center infrastructure. The data suggests policymakers and tech firms must address energy use, privacy, and job displacement to build trust.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50151771' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(442) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://antelang.org/blog/safe_cyclic_mutability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Borrow Checking Zero-Cost Cyclic Mutability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>借用检查：零成本的循环可变性</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50151771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50151771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Describes a borrow-checking design that supports cyclic mutability with zero cost, enabling safe cyclic data structures without a garbage collector. The approach aims to combine Rust‑like safety with the ergonomics of mutable cycles, preserving performance. If validated, it could broaden what you can model without sacrificing speed.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍一种零成本的借用检查设计，支持循环可变性，能够在无垃圾回收的情况下安全实现循环数据结构。该思路力求在保持 Rust 式安全性的同时，提供可变循环的易用性与高性能。如果落地，或将扩展在不牺牲性能前提下的建模能力。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新日期大丰收重新书写印加史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50151447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 34</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50151447' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New dating results push forward or rewrite the chronology of Inca history, altering our understanding of pre-Columbian South America. The findings may shift timelines for state formation and cultural development in the region.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新的放射性年代数据推动并可能重写印加历史的时间线，改变对南美地区前哥伦布时期的理解。研究结果可能改变该地区国家形成与文化发展的时间框架。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(449) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://coveringclimatenow.org/from-us-story/when-the-term-heatwave-is-no-longer-enough/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When the Term &quot;Heatwave&quot; Is No Longer Enough</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为气候变化让极端高温超出传统“热浪”一词的描述，需要用更丰富的指标来表达强度、持续时间和区域风险，并呼吁改进传播与应对。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50214185' data-tags='AI,Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(454) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/microsoft-says-its-new-ai-is-more-than-twice-as-good-at-diagnosing-diseases-as-doctors-2000829562' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New AI Is More Than Twice as Good at Diagnosing Diseases as Doctors</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新一代AI在疾病诊断方面的准确性超过医生两倍以上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50214185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50214185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New AI is more than twice as good at diagnosing diseases, according to the report. While promising for decision support and faster triage, deploying such systems requires rigorous validation and governance to avoid risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，新一代AI在疾病诊断方面的准确性超过医生两倍以上。尽管对决策支持和快速分诊具有潜在价值，但部署此类系统需要严格的验证与治理以降低风险。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(456) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xscreensaver-official/xscreensaver' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There is no official XScreenSaver GitHub repository</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>没有官方的 XScreenSaver GitHub 仓库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50163142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>XScreenSaver lacks an official GitHub repository, leaving users to rely on forks and mirrors. The situation highlights how long-running open-source projects drift from centralized hosting, complicating updates and issue tracking.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>XScreenSaver 缺乏官方 GitHub 仓库，用户只能依赖 forks 与镜像。本质上反映了长期运行的开源项目可能渐渐远离集中托管，更新与问题跟踪变得更加困难。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1422399929/e56328fe-587d-43de-bbc1-b5127c281a97' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1422399929/e56328fe-587d-43de-bbc1-b5127c281a97' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50206361' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(457) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.alexewerlof.com/p/rise-and-fall-of-jev-eval-hype-vs-llm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Rise and Fall of Jev</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jev 的兴衰：评估工具的热潮与落幕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50206361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50206361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jev was a widely touted evaluation framework for LLMs, but its hype faded as practical limits and reproducibility concerns surfaced. The piece traces how initial excitement gave way to skepticism about metrics that failed to capture real-world performance. The takeaway for builders is to anchor evaluation in robust, context-aware benchmarks rather than flashy claims.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jev 曾被视为评估大模型的关键框架，但随着实际局限性和可重复性问题浮现，其热度逐渐退去。文章梳理了从最初的 hype 到对衡量标准的质疑的转变，强调单靠表面指标难以反映真实性能。对开发者而言，应以稳健、情境化的基准来取代夸张的性能承诺。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Ek1R!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd1bcb526-45f1-4966-9fbf-af054e8a637b_931x614.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Ek1R!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd1bcb526-45f1-4966-9fbf-af054e8a637b_931x614.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Time Nuts is a community-driven primer on precise timekeeping, covering tools, methods, and practical tips for calibrating clocks, GPS, and NTP. The writeup highlights why accurate time is foundational for many tech systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Time Nuts 是一个关于精确时序的社区入门，涵盖工具、方法以及标定时钟、GPS 与 NTP 的实用要点。介绍强调了准确时间对众多科技系统的基础作用。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50223752' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(464) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/sciadv.aeh8870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Glymphatic clearance to alleviate brain edema and Alzheimer&#x27;s pathology in mice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>糖淋巴清除可缓解小鼠脑水肿与阿尔茨海默病病理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50223752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50223752' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A mouse study shows that enhancing glymphatic clearance reduces brain edema and Alzheimer&#x27;s pathology, suggesting a potential therapeutic angle for neurodegenerative diseases. If translated to humans, it could influence sleep-related clearance strategies and drug delivery approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>小鼠研究显示，增强糖淋巴系统清除能力可减少脑水肿并缓解阿尔茨海默病病理，提示神经退行性疾病治疗的新方向。如果转化到人类，可能影响与睡眠相关的清除机制及药物递送策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50156779' data-tags='Show HN,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(466) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dovepass.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Dovepass – can I legally fly a drone along this exact path in SF?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Dovepass——我可以在旧金山沿这条具体路径合法飞无人机吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50156779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50156779' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 8</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dovepass provides a way to check the legality of flying a drone along a precise route in San Francisco, aggregating FAA and local rules. It helps pilots plan compliant, risk-aware flights.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Dovepass 提供按路线检查无人机飞行是否合法的工具，整合 FAA 与地方规定，帮助飞行员规划合规且更低风险的航线。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>基于3亿局的海量数据分析，揭示了在大富翁游戏中最有效的策略组合。通过概率、资源管理和玩家行为的综合考量，给玩家与游戏设计者提供可操作的洞见。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pragtical v3.14 adds menus, floating views, and session management to a Lua-based editor, broadening its UI and workflow options. The Lua scripting foundation keeps it lightweight and highly customizable for power users. This release signals steady maturation of a niche but expressive editor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pragtical v3.14 增添了菜单、浮动视图和会话管理，扩展了编辑器的界面与工作流选项。基于 Lua 的脚手架保持轻量级与高度可定制，适合偏好定制的高级用户。此次版本释放显示出这一小众编辑器生态的持续成熟。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(469) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://xingjianbai.com/reactive-optimizers/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reactive Optimizers: rewrite the optimizer&#x27;s source code during training</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Reactive Optimizers：训练中动态重写优化器源码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50188440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 27</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50188440' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reactive Optimizers describe a framework that rewrites the optimizer’s code during training to adapt its behavior on the fly. The approach promises potential gains in convergence and robustness, but raises questions about reproducibility and debugging in ML workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Reactive Optimizers 提出一套训练过程中动态重写优化器行为的框架。该方法有望提升收敛性与鲁棒性，但也给可重复性与调试带来挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(470) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/information-technology/2026/10/reactos-reaches-major-milestone-with-ntfs-install-support/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ReactOS reaches major milestone with NTFS install support</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ReactOS 在 NTFS 安装支持方面取得重大里程碑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50205531' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ReactOS now supports installing from NTFS-formatted drives, marking a major milestone in Windows-compatible OS progress. The milestone reduces one of the long-standing barriers for users testing the OS and expands compatibility scope, though overall progress remains incremental.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ReactOS 现在支持从 NTFS 格式的驱动器安装，标志着与 Windows 兼容的进展进入一个重要里程碑。此更新降低了测试该操作系统的门槛，拓展了兼容性，但整体进展仍然是渐进的。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50197450' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员展示迷你脑类器官对点字模式的理解，向把活组织与数字系统连接的生物混合计算迈出关键一步。这一进展推动了神经启发接口的发展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(473) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/AmadeusITGroup/Edison' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parsing EDIFACT in Python with syntax trees</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50195535' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Edison provides a Python toolkit to parse EDIFACT messages using syntax trees, enabling more readable, verifiable parsing of standardized EDI data. This approach helps developers validate and transform large batch files used in travel, logistics, and commerce.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Edison 提供了一个用语法树解析 EDIFACT 的 Python 工具包，使对标准化EDI数据的解析更加直观且可验证。该方法有助于在旅行业、物流和商业领域处理的大规模文件进行验证与转换。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50224691' data-tags='Energy,Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(478) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/features/2026-10-23/nigeria-embraces-solar-energy-as-panels-become-popular-on-wedding-registries' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In Nigeria, Solar Panels Are the New Wedding Gift</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在尼日利亚，太阳能板成了新婚礼物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50224691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50224691' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Solar panels have become a popular wedding gift in Nigeria as households pursue reliable, off-grid electricity. The trend signals accelerating energy access at the grassroots level and could reshape consumer finance, supply chains, and local entrepreneurship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尼日利亚的婚礼礼物清单中，太阳能板正成为高人气选项，帮助家庭获得稳定的离网电力。这个趋势可能推动基层用电普及、改变消费金融与供应链格局，并促进本地创业。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fedora can leverage TPM2 for hardware-backed full-disk encryption with LUKS, enabling secure automatic unlocking and tamper-resistance at boot.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在 Fedora 上，利用 TPM2 为全盘加密（LUKS）提供硬件支持，实现启动时的自动解锁与防篡改特性。文中解释了配置要点、潜在注意事项，并给出在实际环境中部署 TPM2 全盘加密的步骤。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.computerworld.com/article/4239858/google-will-pay-people-to-use-less-electricity-so-its-data-centers-can-use-more.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google will pay people to use less electricity so its data centers can use more</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲夏季热浪在城市区域造成超过5万人死亡</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Europe’s summer heat wave killed more than 50,000 people in urban areas, underscoring urban vulnerability to extreme heat and the need for adaptive infrastructure, heat warnings, and public health readiness. The death toll highlights climate risk in densely populated areas.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲夏季热浪在城市区域造成超过5万人的死亡，凸显城市对极端高温的脆弱性以及对适应性基础设施、热警报和公共卫生准备的迫切需求。死亡人数凸显了高密度人口区域的气候风险。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/sam-altman-i-no-longer-think-were-gonna-be-able-to-just-patch-all-the-bugs-2000829947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sam Altman: &#x27;I No Longer Think We&#x27;re Gonna Be Able to Just Patch All the Bugs&#x27;</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sam Altman argues that patching AI systems won&#x27;t suffice to fix all issues, signaling a turn toward deeper safety mechanisms and design improvements. The remark implies a push for robust verification, governance, and architectural changes beyond quick fixes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>萨姆·奥特曼表示，仅靠修补不足以解决 AI 系统的所有问题，暗示需要更深层次的安全机制、设计改进以及更完善的治理和体系结构变革。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(489) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.pushsquare.com/news/2026/10/sony-exits-uhd-blu-ray-player-market-as-pull-back-from-physical-media-continues' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sony Exits UHD Blu-ray Player Market as Pull Back from Physical Media Continues</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>索尼退出 UHD Blu-ray 播放器市场，实体媒体收缩继续</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50201821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50201821' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony is retreating from the UHD Blu-ray player market as consumers shift toward streaming and digital libraries. The move underscores a broader decline of physical media hardware and implications for suppliers and collectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>索尼宣布退出 UHD Blu-ray 播放器市场，消费者正转向流媒体与数字化库。此举凸显实体媒体硬件的普遍下滑及对供应商与收藏者的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50236828' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(490) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.businessinsider.com/apollo-ai-zero-financial-payoff-corporate-america-economic-analysis-2026-10' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apollo says AI has delivered &#x27;zero financial payoff&#x27; outside the tech industry</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apollo AI 认为 AI 在科技领域外尚未实现经济回报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50236828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50236828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apollo AI contends that AI has delivered measurable value inside corporate tech but has yet to show clear financial payoff in non-tech industries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apollo AI 指出 AI 已在企业科技领域带来可衡量的价值，但在非科技行业尚未显现明确的经济回报。文章讨论 ROI 障碍、集成挑战，以及 AI 仍可能在哪些领域显现价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.insider.com/6ade1f21ab741a2ebdf111c1?width=1200&amp;format=jpeg' data-full='https://i.insider.com/6ade1f21ab741a2ebdf111c1?width=1200&amp;format=jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50235639' data-tags='Startups,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(491) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://focused.io/operating-principles' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Focused Company Operating Principles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>聚焦型公司的运营原则</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50235639' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Focused Company Operating Principles offers a compact playbook for aligning teams around core values, decision rights, and measurable constraints. It borrows startup governance ideas to help teams stay focused and move fast with clarity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Focused 的运营原则提供了一份简短的行动指南，帮助团队围绕核心价值、决策权和可衡量的约束达成一致，借鉴初创治理思路以提升专注度与执行力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6915b44a5861a2536d561406/693948bbdff3951507d041ea_focused-og-image.webp' data-full='https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6915b44a5861a2536d561406/693948bbdff3951507d041ea_focused-og-image.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50221218' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(492) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/10/23/nx-s1-6009527/monkey-macaque-germs-virus-outbreak' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Will a macaque eat a grape sitting on a pile of poo? A study on germophobia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>猕猴会在粪堆上吃葡萄吗？关于洁癖的研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50221218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50221218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study examines whether macaques avoid food contaminated with feces, shedding light on primate germophobia and risk assessment in social groups. The results inform our understanding of disease-avoidance behaviors in non-human primates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项研究探讨猕猴在面对被粪便污染的食物时是否会回避，揭示灵长类动物的洁癖行为与群体中的风险评估。研究结果有助于理解非人灵长类的疾病回避行为。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/4262x2397+0+222/resize/1400/quality/85/format/webp/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fd2%2Fdc%2F65aa0401457d8d413311a90b4d77%2Fmacaque-5355.jpg' data-full='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/4262x2397+0+222/resize/1400/quality/85/format/webp/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fd2%2Fdc%2F65aa0401457d8d413311a90b4d77%2Fmacaque-5355.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50222826' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.skyvern.com/blog/deleting-rag-from-our-web-agent-made-it-2-3x-faster/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Progressive disclosure in agents to make them 2.3x faster</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理加速：在代理中采用分阶段披露将速度提升 2.3 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50222826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50222826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Removing a bottleneck (rag) and applying progressive disclosure boosted a web agent’s speed by about 2.3x. The result demonstrates how targeted architectural tweaks can yield outsized gains without rewriting core systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>消除瓶颈并采用分阶段披露后，网页代理的速度提升约 2.3 倍。这一结果展示了通过有针对性的架构优化，在无需重写核心系统的情况下实现显著性能提升的可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dcbllm8dvghjo.cloudfront.net/media/blog/88511de5574cf5bdd2a04426b5fb92e4394d73fddd33bdde9b6d9e76d8d1be06-e6419c153de022a93d8dfbb3008ad782a8492104942ce5b77c302742291b9f51-odysseys-accuracy-vs-steps.png' data-full='https://dcbllm8dvghjo.cloudfront.net/media/blog/88511de5574cf5bdd2a04426b5fb92e4394d73fddd33bdde9b6d9e76d8d1be06-e6419c153de022a93d8dfbb3008ad782a8492104942ce5b77c302742291b9f51-odysseys-accuracy-vs-steps.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(494) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://proton.me/flock-camera-map' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Proton Flock Camera Map</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50236748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50236748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proton Flock Camera Map is an interactive visualization showing camera coverage and locations, illustrating the balance between surveillance transparency and privacy. It gives readers a geospatial sense of where cameras are concentrated and how policy shapes visibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Proton Flock 摄像头地图是一个交互式可视化，展示摄像头覆盖范围与分布，揭示监控透明性与隐私之间的权衡，并以地理信息呈现摄像头集中的区域及相关政策的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50223559' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(496) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Linux-7.4-memblock' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Linux&#x27;s Early Boot-Time Physical Memory Allocator Big Speed-Up: ~268s to ~50ms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 启动阶段物理内存分配器实现巨幅提速：从约268秒到约50毫秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50223559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50223559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new early-boot physical memory allocator slashes boot time dramatically, reducing initial memory setup from minutes to milliseconds. The change improves boot reliability and responsiveness and highlights ongoing kernel memory-management innovations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种新的启动阶段物理内存分配器将启动时间显著缩短，从数分钟降至毫秒级，提升启动可靠性和响应速度，凸显内核内存管理领域的持续创新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=linux_74_memblock' data-full='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=linux_74_memblock' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50234699' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(497) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vithushansylvester.com/writing/the-real-impact-of-ai-on-tech-jobs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI didn&#x27;t cause the tech jobs bust, but it explains why there&#x27;s no recovery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 不是造成科技岗位下滑的原因，但解释了为何尚无复苏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50234699' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI didn’t cause the tech jobs bust, but it explains why there’s no immediate rebound—structural labor-market factors, long retraining cycles, and investment lags slow hiring in tech. The piece advocates targeted upskilling and supportive policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 并非导致科技岗位下滑的直接原因，但解释了为何短期内尚无复苏——劳动力市场的结构性因素、再培训周期长、投资回暖滞后等在科技领域放慢了招聘。文中倡导有针对性的技能提升和政策支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vithushansylvester.com/writing/the-real-impact-of-ai-on-tech-jobs/opengraph-image?66fbde5ab7d04a0f' data-full='https://vithushansylvester.com/writing/the-real-impact-of-ai-on-tech-jobs/opengraph-image?66fbde5ab7d04a0f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(498) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ediblegeography.com/a-cocktail-party-in-the-street-an-interview-with-alan-stillman/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Cocktail Party in the Street: An Interview with Alan Stillman (2010)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>街头鸡尾酒派对：对 Alan Stillman 的访谈（2010）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50185715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 7</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50185715' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This 2010 interview revisits Stillman’s approach to social life and urban spaces, shedding light on the origins of casual street gatherings that helped shape modern nightlife culture. It offers timeless observations on creating inclusive social spaces and entrepreneurship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇2010年的访谈回顾 Stillman 对社会生活与城市空间的理念，揭示了催生现代夜生活文化的街头鸡尾酒派对的起源。文章传达了关于打造包容性社交空间与创业精神的持续洞见。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(499) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sfgate.com/bayarea/article/government-spy-plane-49ers-22485781.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U-2 spy plane makes rare appearance in Bay Area flyover</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>U-2 侦察机罕见现身湾区空中巡游</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50173520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 6</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50173520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U-2 spy plane made a rare Bay Area flyover, underscoring its enduring role in high-altitude reconnaissance and the ongoing relevance of legacy aircraft in certain missions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>U-2 侦察机罕见现身湾区上空，显示其在高空侦察任务中的长期作用，以及对现代航天安全的隐喻。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(500) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.makeuseof.com/google-nest-thermostats-unsupported/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google has officially turned these Nest thermostats back into dumb thermostats</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌已将这批 Nest 恒温器重新改为普通恒温器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50226434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50226434' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google has turned certain Nest thermostats back into dumb thermostats, removing smart features via firmware updates. This reflects supplier risk management and feature rollback decisions that affect consumer experience and privacy expectations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌通过固件更新关闭部分智能功能，将部分 Nest 恒温器改回纯粹的温控设备。这反映出厂商在隐私、功能与兼容性之间的权衡，以及对消费者体验的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static0.makeuseofimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2026/09/nest.jpeg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static0.makeuseofimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2026/09/nest.jpeg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50218743' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(501) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tbray.org/ongoing/When/202x/2026/10/19/The-AI-Temptation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>That AI Temptation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>那场 AI 的诱惑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50218743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50218743' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful take on how AI tempts teams with rapid productivity gains, risking overreliance and deployment of brittle AI systems. The piece argues for disciplined governance and thoughtful risk assessment as AI becomes more embedded in workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对人工智能带来快速生产力提升的诱惑进行深思分析，警惕过度依赖与脆弱的 AI 系统投入。文章主张在 AI 深度嵌入工作流的同时，需进行克制与风险评估的治理。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.tbray.org/ongoing/misc/podcast-default.jpg' data-full='https://www.tbray.org/ongoing/misc/podcast-default.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-50219588' data-tags='Startups,Business,AI,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(502) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.timesofisrael.com/us-israeli-browser-startup-valued-at-6-4-billion-as-ai-agents-reshape-workplace-risk/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US-Israeli browser startup valued at $6.4B</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美以浏览器初创公司估值64亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50219588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A US-Israeli browser startup valued at $6.4B as AI agents reshape workplace productivity and risk management. The funding signals strong investor enthusiasm for AI-assisted browsers that promise smarter workflows and governance features.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家美以浏览器初创公司在 AI 助力工作流程与风险管理的背景下，估值达到 64 亿美元，凸显投资者对 AI 助力的浏览器与生产力工具的热情，以及对工作流程治理功能的期待。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(503) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://semperplay.com/news/operation-raven-origins-and-plan/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Building a multiplayer FPS that runs in the browser, and you can play it now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在浏览器中运行的多人 FPS：现在就能玩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=50189693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-native multiplayer FPS demonstrates high-performance, plugin-free gameplay using modern web tech. The project showcases advances in rendering, networking, and input handling that bring fast, responsive shooters to the browser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个原生浏览器的多人射击游戏在无需插件的情况下实现高性能玩法，展示了渲染、网络和输入处理的进步，使浏览器也能提供快速、灵敏的射击体验。</p>
</div>
